# Vercel React Best Practices

Comprehensive performance optimization guide for React and Next.js applications, maintained by Vercel. Contains 57 rules across 8 categories, prioritized by impact to guide automated refactoring and code generation.

## When to Apply

Reference these guidelines when:
- Writing new React components or Next.js pages
- Implementing data fetching (client or server-side)
- Reviewing code for performance issues
- Refactoring existing React/Next.js code
- Optimizing bundle size or load times

## Rule Categories by Priority

| Priority | Category | Impact | Prefix |
|----------|----------|--------|--------|
| 1 | Eliminating Waterfalls | CRITICAL | `async-` |
| 2 | Bundle Size Optimization | CRITICAL | `bundle-` |
| 3 | Server-Side Performance | HIGH | `server-` |
| 4 | Client-Side Data Fetching | MEDIUM-HIGH | `client-` |
| 5 | Re-render Optimization | MEDIUM | `rerender-` |
| 6 | Rendering Performance | MEDIUM | `rendering-` |
| 7 | JavaScript Performance | LOW-MEDIUM | `js-` |
| 8 | Advanced Patterns | LOW | `advanced-` |

## Quick Reference

### 1. Eliminating Waterfalls (CRITICAL)

- `async-defer-await` - Move await into branches where actually used
- `async-parallel` - Use Promise.all() for independent operations
- `async-dependencies` - Use better-all for partial dependencies
- `async-api-routes` - Start promises early, await late in API routes
- `async-suspense-boundaries` - Use Suspense to stream content

### 2. Bundle Size Optimization (CRITICAL)

- `bundle-barrel-imports` - Import directly, avoid barrel files
- `bundle-dynamic-imports` - Use next/dynamic for heavy components
- `bundle-defer-third-party` - Load analytics/logging after hydration
- `bundle-conditional` - Load modules only when feature is activated
- `bundle-preload` - Preload on hover/focus for perceived speed

### 3. Server-Side Performance (HIGH)

- `server-auth-actions` - Authenticate server actions like API routes
- `server-cache-react` - Use React.cache() for per-request deduplication
- `server-cache-lru` - Use LRU cache for cross-request caching
- `server-dedup-props` - Avoid duplicate serialization in RSC props
- `server-serialization` - Minimize data passed to client components
- `server-parallel-fetching` - Restructure components to parallelize fetches
- `server-after-nonblocking` - Use after() for non-blocking operations

### 4. Client-Side Data Fetching (MEDIUM-HIGH)

- `client-swr-dedup` - Use SWR for automatic request deduplication
- `client-event-listeners` - Deduplicate global event listeners
- `client-passive-event-listeners` - Use passive listeners for scroll
- `client-localstorage-schema` - Version and minimize localStorage data

### 5. Re-render Optimization (MEDIUM)

- `rerender-defer-reads` - Don't subscribe to state only used in callbacks
- `rerender-memo` - Extract expensive work into memoized components
- `rerender-memo-with-default-value` - Hoist default non-primitive props
- `rerender-dependencies` - Use primitive dependencies in effects
- `rerender-derived-state` - Subscribe to derived booleans, not raw values
- `rerender-derived-state-no-effect` - Derive state during render, not effects
- `rerender-functional-setstate` - Use functional setState for stable callbacks
- `rerender-lazy-state-init` - Pass function to useState for expensive values
- `rerender-simple-expression-in-memo` - Avoid memo for simple primitives
- `rerender-move-effect-to-event` - Put interaction logic in event handlers
- `rerender-transitions` - Use startTransition for non-urgent updates
- `rerender-use-ref-transient-values` - Use refs for transient frequent values

### 6. Rendering Performance (MEDIUM)

- `rendering-animate-svg-wrapper` - Animate div wrapper, not SVG element
- `rendering-content-visibility` - Use content-visibility for long lists
- `rendering-hoist-jsx` - Extract static JSX outside components
- `rendering-svg-precision` - Reduce SVG coordinate precision
- `rendering-hydration-no-flicker` - Use inline script for client-only data
- `rendering-hydration-suppress-warning` - Suppress expected mismatches
- `rendering-activity` - Use Activity component for show/hide
- `rendering-conditional-render` - Use ternary, not && for conditionals
- `rendering-usetransition-loading` - Prefer useTransition for loading state

### 7. JavaScript Performance (LOW-MEDIUM)

- `js-batch-dom-css` - Group CSS changes via classes or cssText
- `js-index-maps` - Build Map for repeated lookups
- `js-cache-property-access` - Cache object properties in loops
- `js-cache-function-results` - Cache function results in module-level Map
- `js-cache-storage` - Cache localStorage/sessionStorage reads
- `js-combine-iterations` - Combine multiple filter/map into one loop
- `js-length-check-first` - Check array length before expensive comparison
- `js-early-exit` - Return early from functions
- `js-hoist-regexp` - Hoist RegExp creation outside loops
- `js-min-max-loop` - Use loop for min/max instead of sort
- `js-set-map-lookups` - Use Set/Map for O(1) lookups
- `js-tosorted-immutable` - Use toSorted() for immutability

### 8. Advanced Patterns (LOW)

- `advanced-event-handler-refs` - Store event handlers in refs
- `advanced-init-once` - Initialize app once per app load
- `advanced-use-latest` - useLatest for stable callback refs

## How to Use

Read individual rule files for detailed explanations and code examples:

```
rules/async-parallel.md
rules/bundle-barrel-imports.md
```

Each rule file contains:
- Brief explanation of why it matters
- Incorrect code example with explanation
- Correct code example with explanation
- Additional context and references

## Full Compiled Document

For the complete guide with all rules expanded: `AGENTS.md`

# Backend Conventions

## Async Safety
- **MinIO calls MUST use `asyncio.to_thread()`** in async endpoints. MinIO client is sync (urllib3). Direct calls block event loop; when MinIO is unreachable, blocks ALL requests for 30+s. Client configured with short timeouts (connect=5s, read=10s, 2 retries)
- **Celery uses sync DB** (`psycopg`), API uses async (`asyncpg`). Never mix.

## Credits & Billing
- **Two-stage debit**: ① Pre-check balance (402 if insufficient) → ② `debit_credits()` pre-debits estimated cost (returns ledger ID) → stream → `reconcile_credits()` UPDATEs same ledger entry to actual cost. Single ledger record per chat. LLM failure → DELETE entry + full refund
- **Durable settlement (v0.24.0, Codex-consensus)**: `credit_ledger.reconciled_at` is the settlement marker. `reconcile_credits()` takes `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` and ALWAYS stamps `reconciled_at` — including the equal-cost no-op path. Every refund is a single atomic conditional `DELETE ... WHERE reconciled_at IS NULL RETURNING id`; rowcount 0 = money already settled = no refund. ALL final-commit exceptions (not just `CancelledError`) route through the marker resolver; resolver failure must NEVER fall through to a blind refund (leave predebit standing, log `*.unresolved`). Do not reintroduce read-then-act refund logic.
- Quote search billing: `reason="quote_search"`, predebit 15 (balanced estimate), reconcile to actual; verified-empty results charge actual cost. Chat-routed quote searches bill through the chat message's own ledger row (predebit forced to 15 when the strict trigger fires, regardless of selected mode) — never a second row.
- **`ChatRequest` exposes only `mode` field** (`quick`/`balanced`; legacy `thorough` is retired). `model` field removed — prevents billing bypass
- Stripe webhook: `checkout.session.completed` for subscriptions only updates plan (no credits); `invoice.payment_succeeded` grants monthly credits (idempotent by invoice.id)
- `POST /api/billing/cancel` is self-serve and records optional `cancel_reason`, `cancel_feedback`, and `refund_requested` metadata in `plan_transitions`. `refund_requested` is an internal review flag; do not issue Stripe refunds from this path unless an explicit refund workflow is added.

## Parse Worker
- `time_limit=600`, `soft_time_limit=540`, `autoretry_for=(Exception,)`, max 2 retries, 60s backoff
- Idempotent re-parse: **delete Qdrant vectors (by `document_id` filter) BEFORE deleting DB pages/chunks**. Ordering matters — a Qdrant outage must leave the existing rows intact (set error + return), else the two stores diverge / data is lost. Then re-index.
- **PDF page text is persisted forward-only (v0.24.0)**: the extract pass stores `page.get_text("text")` per page into `pages.content` (previously NULL for all PDFs). Legacy docs keep NULL until re-parsed. `get_document_text_content` uses page mode ONLY when coverage is complete and consecutive (`1..page_count`, all non-blank); otherwise chunk fallback.
- **OCR trigger = `detect_scanned` (no text layer) OR `detect_low_quality_text` (PDF text layer present but garbled — broken-font cmap, Unicode-aware quality score)**. R2b fix for docs like U13 that have garbage text and so were never detected as "scanned".
- **OCR language is content-based**: `detect_script_osd` runs `tesseract --psm 0` (OSD) on sample pages → `resolve_ocr_languages(locale, script)` returns a NARROW set (script family, ≤3, **no `eng` for non-Latin** — it injects Latin noise). Never the kitchen-sink set (causes cross-script hallucination); locale only refines within a script family. Adopt a low-quality re-OCR only if it beats the text-layer quality. Persist `parse_version`/`parse_method`/`text_quality`/`ocr_languages` on the doc.
- Backfill stale/low-quality docs with `scripts/find_low_quality_docs.py` (skips `parse_version>=current` unless `--force`).

## Verified Quote Pipeline (M2, v0.24.0 — Codex 6-round consensus; do not weaken)
- **The guarantee**: a quote card is NEVER rendered from LLM-emitted text. `verify_quote()` (M1 substrate) gates every proposal; display text is ALWAYS the raw source slice. Flagged-tier (fuzzy 90–95) results are discarded from cards, only counted.
- **Verification source** (`quote_source_service`): all pages in the chunk's range have `Page.content` → per-page verification, kind=`page_text`; else cited chunk ± neighbors, kind=`extracted_text`. Trust labels derive from kind and are honest per-kind (word-for-word claim only for `page_text`).
- **Page attribution derives from the VERIFIED slice** (plan §8.1): multi-page `extracted_text` segments are DISCARDED (`ambiguous_page_range`); `page_text` duplicates emit one card per matching page. Never attribute via majority-bbox voting.
- **Chat routing is deterministic-safe**: auto-route to the billed pipeline ONLY when the strict trigger matches AND zero negation/metalinguistic tokens appear anywhere in the message; otherwise the ordinary RAG path runs with `quote_finder_hint`/`quote_finder_topic` on the SSE `done` event (frontend chip). Guarded triggers FORCE the RAG path — never a tool action. Do not re-attempt regex intent-scope resolution; the policy is adjudicated (asymmetric loss).
- **Saved quotes re-verify server-side**: the save endpoint accepts only `chunk_id + quote_text` and re-derives tier/score/page/kind via `verify_saved_quote()`; client-supplied trust fields would forge "verified" cards. Fabrication = 422 `QUOTE_NOT_VERIFIABLE`. Saved rows snapshot trust fields at save time (survive reparses; `source_chunk_id` is ON DELETE SET NULL).
- Caps: `FREE_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT=30` counts ACTIVE rows per user across documents; delete frees a slot; idempotent re-saves are never capped.

## Auth
- **`FOR UPDATE` lock** on verification tokens to prevent TOCTOU
- Internal Auth Adapter API uses `X-Adapter-Secret` header

## Error Handling
- Use `HTTPException` (not `JSONResponse`) for all non-SSE endpoints
- Lifespan pattern (`@asynccontextmanager`) instead of deprecated `@app.on_event`

## Demo System
- 3 seed PDFs auto-deployed at startup from `backend/seed_data/`. Self-healing covers BOTH stores: Qdrant vector loss → full re-seed; missing MinIO objects → `_ensure_demo_files` stats each doc's `storage_key` and re-uploads from seed_data (id/key-preserving). Added after the 2026-08 MinIO-v2 migration silently lost ~106/108 stored files (chat worked, PDF pane didn't). Seed assets are immutable per slug — the stat→put TOCTOU is accepted on that invariant.
- Anonymous limits (v0.23.0): **5 msgs per (IP, document) per 24h** (matches marketing copy), session cap = 500 per doc counted over a **24h rolling window of anonymous sessions only**, 10 req/min/IP, forced DeepSeek V4 Flash. Nightly beat task prunes empty demo sessions >7d (anon AND authed).
- Free-plan authed users get a per-user session cap on demo docs (`FREE_MAX_SESSIONS_PER_DOC`, own sessions only) — closes the row-spam DoS on the anonymous cap.
- Logged-in users accessing demo docs use their credits with no message limit

## Testing
- **Integration tests NEVER touch the shared dev DB.** `tests/conftest.py` forces a scratch `doctalk_test` database (auto-provisioned + migrated) and hard-refuses non-loopback hosts unless `DOCTALK_TEST_DATABASE_URL` is explicitly set. Two dev-DB wipe incidents (2026-08-02: alembic downgrade-base; integration fixtures) led to this — do not weaken it back to setdefault/conditional form. Never run `alembic downgrade` against `doctalk`.

# Frontend Conventions

## Architecture
- **All pages are `"use client"`** — client components with server wrapper for metadata
- Pages that fetch API data must render meaningful content in loading AND error states (prevents Google Soft 404)

## API Proxy
- **ALL** frontend→backend calls go through `/api/proxy/*` route, which injects JWT. Including SSE chat stream (`sse.ts`). Missing this = 401 errors
- **JWT double-layer**: Auth.js uses encrypted JWE (unreadable by backend). Proxy creates plain HS256 JWT via `jose`. Backend `deps.py` validates exp/iat/sub
- `allowDangerousEmailAccountLinking: true` enables cross-provider auto-linking by email
- **Proxy maxDuration**: `route.ts` exports `maxDuration = 60` (Vercel Hobby limit). SSE chat 60s timeout, others 30s

## UI Design System
- **Palette (app UI)**: zinc monochrome + blue accent (`#1D4ED8`/`#60A5FA`). Zero `gray-*`/`indigo-*`/`violet-*`/`purple-*` classes (except Google OAuth brand + status colors). Zero `transition-all` (use specific properties)
- **Editorial marketing layer**: the entire public marketing surface (unauthenticated `/`, `use-cases/*`, `compare/*`, `alternatives/*`, `features/*`, `tools/*`, `pricing`, `trust`, `demo`) uses a SEPARATE scoped editorial design system — `frontend/src/app/editorial.css` (every rule under `.dt-editorial`), a warm-paper palette (`--ed-paper`/`--ed-ink`/`--ed-signal` terracotta `#b0472f`/`--ed-ochre`) with Newsreader serif + IBM Plex Mono fonts, **light-only**. It does NOT use the zinc/blue app palette. **Design decision locked 2026-05-20**: the product runs on TWO surface treatments (editorial marketing terracotta+warm-paper vs functional app zinc+blue) sharing one token base (logo, body font Inter, spacing scale, micro-interactions). A blue-accent unification was tried and reverted because the warm-paper terracotta identity is load-bearing. Do not re-propose merging the accents. Marketing pages compose the shared editorial kit in `frontend/src/components/marketing/` (`MarketingShell`, `EditorialMarketingHeader`, `EdPageHero`, `EdSection`, `EdProse`, `EdFeatureList`, `EdCardGrid`, `EdStepRow`, `EdFaqList`, `EdCtaBanner`, `EdComparisonTable`, `EdInlineCell`, `EdRelatedLinks`, `EdCheckList`, `EdChoiceList`) — `MarketingShell` supplies the `.dt-editorial` root, so kit components never add it themselves. Keep editorial styles scoped under `.dt-editorial`; do not let them leak into the functional app UI, and do not apply the zinc/blue rule to editorial components. Pages still on the zinc/blue app palette: `about`, `contact`, `imprint`, `privacy`, `terms`, `blog/*` (document-diff was editorialized in 2026-05; `DocumentDiffPanel` takes `surface="app"|"editorial"` and is the one sanctioned dual-surface component).
- **De-glass leftovers are a bug class**: commit `0b7404a` flattened the CSS but left dark-glass Tailwind utilities in JSX; ~40 invisible-on-white sites were fixed in v0.23.0. When touching app-surface JSX, any `*-white/NN` or bare `text-white`/`hover:text-white` on a light surface needs a light-mode variant (`dark:` keeps the old value). Theme-inverting solids (`bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-zinc-50 dark:text-zinc-900`) are correct as-is.
- **i18n**: Components using `t()` MUST be inside `<LocaleProvider>`. Outside = raw key fallback. Only `en` is statically loaded; other 10 locales lazy-loaded. Localized server pages seed `<LocaleProvider initialLocale initialMessages>` (see `app/[locale]/page.tsx` + `getScopedMessages`) so SSR HTML is translated — a `[locale]` page without seeding ships English first paint (the exact failure the locale-URL program exists to fix).

## Demo Counter & Session Reuse (v0.23.0 — Codex 6-round consensus; do not re-break)
- Contract: `totalUsed = demoMessagesUsed (server count at last restore/create) + (transcript user msgs − demoRestoredUserMsgCount baseline)`. Counters reset ONLY in `useChatSession`'s documentId-keyed effect (NOT `clearDocumentTransientState` — its effect reruns on locale change and wiped the baseline).
- Anonymous demo sessions are reused via `sessionStorage["dt-demo-session:"+docId]` (helper `demoSessionStorage.ts`); pointer cleared only on 404/403; transient adoption failure sets `sessionError` and STOPS (no create fall-through).
- Failed regenerate/continue re-anchors to server truth (`GET messages` → `demo_messages_used`) guarded by sessionId AND `demoAccountingEpoch` (monotonic across `reset()`; bumped at every accounting mutation incl. A→A session switch). Late resolves are dropped, never written.

## Quote Finder UI (M2/M3)
- `QuoteFinderPanel` (reader toolbar entry; anon sees a sign-in CTA instead) + chat `quote_search` artifact + "Try Quote Finder" chip (renders when the SSE `done` event carries `quote_finder_hint`/`quote_finder_topic`; opens the panel with topic prefilled, NEVER auto-submits — searches are billed).
- Trust copy is per-kind and uses the WEAKEST kind present: word-for-word claim only for `page_text` results; `extracted_text` cards carry the amber hyphenation caveat. Do not reintroduce an unconditional verbatim claim.
- Panel resets topic/result/error/loading on every open/retarget with a generation guard (late responses from a prior open are dropped).
- Save button state must not be disabled from a cached count — the cap only blocks genuinely new saves (idempotent re-saves always succeed).

## PDF & Documents
- **react-pdf v9 CJK**: After upgrading react-pdf/pdfjs-dist, MUST re-copy `cmaps/`, `standard_fonts/`, `pdf.worker.min.mjs` to `public/`. Worker loaded from same-origin (not CDN) for CSP compliance
- **bbox coordinates**: Normalized [0,1], top-left origin. Three citation highlight strategies: ① PDF bbox, ② TextViewer text-snippet match, ③ converted PDF fallback to text-snippet when dummy bbox detected. Quote cards reuse the citation jump with chunk-level bboxes and an explicit "highlight location is approximate" label (plan §8.2).

## Subscriptions & Feature Gating
- Free (300/mo) + Plus (3K/mo, $9.99) + Pro (9K/mo, $19.99). Annual = 20% discount
- Visible modes are Flash and Pro. Internal IDs remain `quick` and `balanced`; retired modes such as `thorough` must migrate to Flash.
- Free includes Flash plus a capped number of Pro answers/month. Export: Plus+ (frontend gated). Custom Instructions: Pro (backend gated). Sessions: Free=3/doc (backend gated). Saved quotes: Free=30 active across documents (backend gated; delete frees a slot). Quote searches cost credits like a Pro (balanced) chat message.
- Credit packs: Boost(500/$3.99), Power(2K/$9.99), Ultra(5K/$19.99)
- Cancellation UI must remain self-serve. The cancel form may collect an optional reason, optional feedback, and a refund-review checkbox, but it must not block cancellation on those fields.

The scoped rules confirm the intended invariants: server-only trust derivation, active-row cap, idempotent saves bypassing the cap, and owner-only mutation. I also found an unrelated untracked review file and will leave it untouched; the audit remains read-only. Next I’m tracing each endpoint through its query predicates and transaction boundaries, then I’ll challenge the UI state model independently.

     6	verification corpus the quote was verified against — page text for

     1	"""Saved-quote CRUD + caps (M3-B2, plan D8 as amended by §8.5 M3 / §8.4
     2	point 2, and §8.1's snapshot-at-save-time requirement — see M3-B3).
     3	
     4	Trust boundary: this module NEVER constructs a SavedQuote from
     5	client-supplied tier/score/bboxes. The API layer must always pass a
     6	QuoteCard produced by quote_search_service.verify_saved_quote() (or by
     7	quote_search() itself), so every persisted trust field traces back through
     8	the SAME verify_quote gate the rest of the system uses. `quote_hash` is
     9	likewise always SERVER-computed here (compute_quote_hash), never accepted
    10	from a caller — see SavedQuote's model docstring for why.
    11	"""
    12	from __future__ import annotations
    13	
    14	import hashlib
    15	import uuid
    16	from typing import Optional
    17	
    18	import sqlalchemy as sa
    19	from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
    20	from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
    21	
    22	from app.core.config import settings
    23	from app.models.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    24	from app.models.tables import Document, SavedQuote, User
    25	from app.services.quote_search_service import QuoteCard
    26	from app.services.quote_verification_service import QUOTE_VERIFIER_VERSION
    27	from app.services.text_normalizer import normalize
    28	
    29	_LIMIT_BY_PLAN = {
    30	    "free": lambda: settings.FREE_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT,
    31	    "plus": lambda: settings.PLUS_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT,
    32	    "pro": lambda: settings.PRO_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT,
    33	}
    34	
    35	
    36	def saved_quotes_limit_for_plan(plan: Optional[str]) -> int:
    37	    """Same dict-lookup convention as documents.py's FREE/PLUS/PRO_MAX_DOCUMENTS
    38	    enforcement (numeric sentinel for "unlimited", not the boolean-gate
    39	    convention layout translation uses)."""
    40	    normalized = (plan or "free").lower()
    41	    getter = _LIMIT_BY_PLAN.get(normalized, _LIMIT_BY_PLAN["free"])
    42	    return int(getter())
    43	
    44	
    45	def compute_quote_hash(quote_text: str, page: int, page_end: int) -> str:
    46	    """SERVER-side dedup/idempotency key (§8.1's dedup-key philosophy,
    47	    scoped to one (user, document) pair via the table's UNIQUE constraint
    48	    rather than needing document_id baked into the hash itself):
    49	    normalized quote text + the VERIFIED page range — never the caller's
    50	    raw text or an unverified page guess. Callers must pass the page/
    51	    page_end that came back from verify_saved_quote()/quote_search(), not
    52	    anything client-supplied, or dedup becomes forgeable (two different
    53	    users' honest saves of the same passage should collide; a client
    54	    claiming a fake page range to dodge the unique constraint must not)."""
    55	    normalized_text, _ = normalize(quote_text or "", fuzzy=True)
    56	    payload = f"{normalized_text}|{page}|{page_end}"
    57	    return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    58	
    59	
    60	async def count_active_saved_quotes(db: AsyncSession, user_id: uuid.UUID) -> int:
    61	    """§8.4 point 2: ACTIVE = currently-existing rows, counted ACROSS all of
    62	    the user's documents. There is no soft-delete flag — a DELETE is what
    63	    "frees a slot" means, so a plain COUNT(*) is always the current truth."""
    64	    result = await db.execute(
    65	        sa.select(sa.func.count()).select_from(SavedQuote).where(SavedQuote.user_id == user_id)
    66	    )
    67	    return int(result.scalar() or 0)
    68	
    69	
    70	async def get_existing_saved_quote(
    71	    db: AsyncSession, *, user_id: uuid.UUID, document_id: uuid.UUID, quote_hash: str,
    72	) -> Optional[SavedQuote]:
    73	    result = await db.execute(
    74	        sa.select(SavedQuote)
    75	        .where(SavedQuote.user_id == user_id)
    76	        .where(SavedQuote.document_id == document_id)
    77	        .where(SavedQuote.quote_hash == quote_hash)
    78	    )
    79	    return result.scalar_one_or_none()
    80	
    81	
    82	async def save_quote(
    83	    db: AsyncSession, *, user: User, document: Document, card: QuoteCard,
    84	) -> tuple[SavedQuote, bool]:
    85	    """Idempotent insert — returns (row, created=False on an idempotent hit).
    86	
    87	    `card` MUST already be the output of a server-side re-verification
    88	    (quote_search_service.verify_saved_quote() or quote_search()'s own
    89	    cards) — every persisted trust column is copied from it as-is, never
    90	    recomputed here and never sourced from raw request data.
    91	
    92	    SELECT-then-INSERT + IntegrityError-retry, same race biblio_service's
    93	    FIX-9 precedent handles: two concurrent identical saves (double-click,
    94	    two tabs) can both SELECT None then both attempt to INSERT — UNIQUE
    95	    (user_id, document_id, quote_hash) stops the loser's commit, and the
    96	    loser recovers by re-fetching the winner's row instead of surfacing a
    97	    raw 500/409.
    98	
    99	    UNLIKE biblio_service's byte-for-byte pattern, the retry re-fetch below
   100	    runs on a FRESH AsyncSessionLocal() session, not the just-rolled-back
   101	    `db` — discovered via real-Postgres asyncio.gather concurrency testing
   102	    (test_saved_quotes_integration.py) that reusing a NullPool-backed
   103	    session for a new query immediately after a rollback races with
   104	    SQLAlchemy's async/greenlet connection-checkout machinery under true
   105	    concurrent load (reproduced standalone outside pytest too; a
   106	    production QueuePool-backed engine did not exhibit this — NullPool is
   107	    what TESTING=1 forces, per app/models/database.py). A fresh session
   108	    sidesteps it unconditionally and matches the independent-session-for-
   109	    post-failure-resolution pattern already used by the billing resolvers
   110	    in chat_service.py / quotes.py.
   111	
   112	    The cap check (count_active_saved_quotes vs saved_quotes_limit_for_plan)
   113	    is the CALLER's responsibility, performed BEFORE this function runs —
   114	    a documented, accepted narrow TOCTOU window exists between that check
   115	    and this insert (two concurrent NEW saves could both pass a
   116	    same-instant cap check), same class of minor race as the existing
   117	    MAX_DOCUMENTS/share-link caps elsewhere in this codebase; low value,
   118	    self-correcting on the next delete, and not given the money-grade
   119	    locking billing races get.
   120	    """
   121	    quote_hash = compute_quote_hash(card.display_text, card.page, card.page_end)
   122	    existing = await get_existing_saved_quote(
   123	        db, user_id=user.id, document_id=document.id, quote_hash=quote_hash
   124	    )
   125	    if existing is not None:
   126	        return existing, False
   127	
   128	    row = SavedQuote(
   129	        user_id=user.id,
   130	        document_id=document.id,
   131	        page=card.page,
   132	        page_end=card.page_end,
   133	        quote_text=card.display_text,
   134	        bboxes=card.bboxes,
   135	        verification_tier=card.tier,
   136	        verification_score=card.score,
   137	        verifier_version=QUOTE_VERIFIER_VERSION,
   138	        source_chunk_id=uuid.UUID(card.chunk_id),
   139	        source_kind=card.source_kind,
   140	        # M3 review addition (plan §8.1 anchor fields, 2026-08-03): copied
   141	        # from the card as-is, same as every other trust field — None
   142	        # whenever the caller's card didn't come from verify_saved_quote()
   143	        # (the column is nullable for exactly that reason).
   144	        source_text_hash=card.source_text_hash,
   145	        quote_start=card.quote_start,
   146	        quote_end=card.quote_end,
   147	        quote_hash=quote_hash,
   148	        note=None,
   149	    )
   150	    db.add(row)
   151	    try:
   152	        await db.commit()
   153	    except IntegrityError:
   154	        await db.rollback()
   155	        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as retry_db:
   156	            winner = await get_existing_saved_quote(
   157	                retry_db, user_id=user.id, document_id=document.id, quote_hash=quote_hash
   158	            )
   159	        if winner is None:
   160	            raise  # not the race we anticipated — a genuine failure
   161	        return winner, False
   162	    return row, True
   163	
   164	
   165	async def list_saved_quotes_for_document(
   166	    db: AsyncSession, *, user_id: uuid.UUID, document_id: uuid.UUID,
   167	) -> list[SavedQuote]:
   168	    result = await db.execute(
   169	        sa.select(SavedQuote)
   170	        .where(SavedQuote.user_id == user_id)
   171	        .where(SavedQuote.document_id == document_id)
   172	        .order_by(SavedQuote.created_at.desc())
   173	    )
   174	    return list(result.scalars().all())
   175	
   176	
   177	async def list_all_saved_quotes(db: AsyncSession, *, user_id: uuid.UUID) -> list[SavedQuote]:
   178	    """The Evidence Board feed — every saved quote for this user, across
   179	    every document, newest first. Uses idx_saved_quotes_user_created."""
   180	    result = await db.execute(
   181	        sa.select(SavedQuote)
   182	        .where(SavedQuote.user_id == user_id)
   183	        .order_by(SavedQuote.created_at.desc())
   184	    )
   185	    return list(result.scalars().all())
   186	
   187	
   188	async def get_owned_saved_quote(
   189	    db: AsyncSession, *, user_id: uuid.UUID, saved_quote_id: uuid.UUID,
   190	) -> Optional[SavedQuote]:
   191	    """Ownership is baked directly into the WHERE clause (sharing.py's
   192	    revoke_share precedent) — a saved quote belonging to another user
   193	    simply doesn't match, so callers get a uniform 404 for both
   194	    "doesn't exist" and "exists but isn't yours," never leaking which."""
   195	    result = await db.execute(
   196	        sa.select(SavedQuote)
   197	        .where(SavedQuote.id == saved_quote_id)
   198	        .where(SavedQuote.user_id == user_id)
   199	    )
   200	    return result.scalar_one_or_none()
   201	
   202	
   203	async def update_note(db: AsyncSession, *, row: SavedQuote, note: Optional[str]) -> SavedQuote:
   204	    """Live E2E bug (team lead, 2026-08-03): PATCH .../quotes/{id} 500'd
   205	    with `sqlalchemy.exc.MissingGreenlet`. Root cause: `updated_at`
   206	    (server-side `onupdate=sa.func.now()`) gets marked EXPIRED by
   207	    SQLAlchemy after this UPDATE's flush — UNLIKE a fresh INSERT (see
   208	    save_quote() above), where INSERT...RETURNING auto-populates
   209	    server_default columns synchronously as part of the flush, an UPDATE's
   210	    onupdate-computed value is NOT auto-refreshed the same way. The
   211	    caller's later SYNCHRONOUS read of that expired attribute
   212	    (`row.updated_at.isoformat()` inside quotes.py's _saved_quote_response,
   213	    which runs as plain Pydantic-model construction, never inside an
   214	    `await`) triggered an implicit lazy DB reload from OUTSIDE an active
   215	    greenlet/await context — exactly MissingGreenlet. `db.refresh(row)`
   216	    here, INSIDE this awaited function, forces that reload to happen
   217	    safely now, so every attribute is a normal in-memory value by the time
   218	    any caller touches it synchronously. Mocked unit tests (row as a bare
   219	    SimpleNamespace) cannot exercise this at all — only a REAL SQLAlchemy
   220	    ORM object has attribute-expiration machinery to trigger it; see
   221	    test_saved_quotes_integration.py's TestSavedQuotesEndpointsRealAsgiRealDb
   222	    for the real-app/real-DB regression coverage."""
   223	    row.note = note
   224	    await db.commit()
   225	    await db.refresh(row)
   226	    return row
   227	
   228	
   229	async def delete_saved_quote(db: AsyncSession, *, row: SavedQuote) -> None:
   230	    await db.delete(row)
   231	    await db.commit()

   780	class SavedQuote(Base):
   781	    """User-saved verified quote cards (M3-B1, plan D8 as amended by §8.5 M3
   782	    + §8.1's verification-persistence requirement).
   783	
   784	    Rows snapshot a card's full trust state AT SAVE TIME
   785	    (tier/score/verifier_version/source_kind, plus bboxes/page/page_end) —
   786	    saved quotes must survive/revalidate after reparses (§8.1), so the
   787	    display path reads these stored columns and NEVER re-runs verify_quote.
   788	    `source_chunk_id` is therefore ON DELETE SET NULL, not CASCADE: the
   789	    parse worker hard-deletes and recreates a document's chunks on every
   790	    reparse (parse_worker.py's `sa_delete(Chunk).where(...)`), and a saved
   791	    quote must outlive that — losing only the (informational) live chunk
   792	    link, never the row itself or its snapshotted display fields.
   793	
   794	    Cap enforcement (§8.4 point 2) counts ACTIVE rows per user ACROSS
   795	    documents — there is no soft-delete flag; DELETE is a real row removal,
   796	    which is what "delete frees a slot" means in the plan.
   797	
   798	    `quote_hash` is SERVER-derived (normalized quote_text + verified page
   799	    range, see saved_quotes_service.compute_quote_hash) — never trust a
   800	    client-supplied hash, or dedup/idempotency becomes forgeable.
   801	    """
   802	    __tablename__ = "saved_quotes"
   803	
   804	    id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
   805	        UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, server_default=sa.text("gen_random_uuid()")
   806	    )
   807	    user_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
   808	        UUID(as_uuid=True), sa.ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False
   809	    )
   810	    document_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
   811	        UUID(as_uuid=True), sa.ForeignKey("documents.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False
   812	    )
   813	    page: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(sa.Integer, nullable=False)
   814	    page_end: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(sa.Integer, nullable=False)
   815	    quote_text: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(sa.Text, nullable=False)
   816	    bboxes: Mapped[Optional[list]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=True)
   817	    verification_tier: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(sa.String(16), nullable=False)
   818	    verification_score: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(sa.Float, nullable=False)
   819	    verifier_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(sa.String(16), nullable=False)
   820	    source_chunk_id: Mapped[Optional[uuid.UUID]] = mapped_column(
   821	        UUID(as_uuid=True), sa.ForeignKey("chunks.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
   822	    )
   823	    source_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(sa.String(16), nullable=False)
   824	    quote_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(sa.String(64), nullable=False)
   825	    # M3 review addition (2026-08-03, plan §8.1's verification-anchor
   826	    # fields, added while the table was still empty in prod — migration
   827	    # 20260803_0037). source_text_hash: hash of the verification corpus
   828	    # (page text for page_text-kind cards, chunk text for extracted_text-
   829	    # kind cards) the quote was verified against. quote_start/quote_end:
   830	    # raw character offsets of the verified slice within that corpus
   831	    # (QuoteVerification.raw_start/raw_end). Purpose: future revalidation
   832	    # after reparses — comparing a freshly-fetched corpus's hash against
   833	    # this stored one tells a later pass whether the text changed since
   834	    # save time. Nullable: v1 populates them at save time via

   836	    # read (M3-B3, unchanged) — nothing reads these columns yet.
   837	    source_text_hash: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(sa.Text, nullable=True)

   839	    quote_end: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(sa.Integer, nullable=True)
   840	    note: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(sa.Text, nullable=True)

    80	async def _verify_document(document_id: uuid.UUID, user: User, db: AsyncSession) -> Document:
    81	    """Access control lives HERE, not in quote_search_service.quote_search().
    82	
    83	    B3's quote_search() takes a `user` param it never reads for access
    84	    control — it trusts the caller already resolved and authorized
    85	    `document`. This endpoint is that caller: it MUST call
    86	    can_access_document() itself before ever invoking quote_search(), same
    87	    as every other document-scoped endpoint (extractions.py, documents.py).
    88	    """
    89	    doc = await db.get(Document, document_id)
    90	    if not doc or not can_access_document(doc, user):
    91	        raise HTTPException(
    92	            status_code=404,
    93	            detail={"error": "DOCUMENT_NOT_FOUND", "message": "Document not found"},
    94	        )
    95	    return doc
    96	
    97	
    98	async def _refund_predebit(db: AsyncSession, user_id: uuid.UUID, pre_debited: int, ledger_id: uuid.UUID) -> bool:
    99	    """Same idempotent, RACE-FREE refund idea as chat_service._refund_predebit,
   100	    NOT a byte-for-byte mirror: that version does its own
   101	    `try: await db.rollback() except: pass` internally before the delete.
   102	    This one does not — callers roll back their OWN session themselves
   103	    first when needed (MINOR-4, review round 1 correction).
   104	
   105	    FIX3-A(c) (Codex r3 #4, NOT ADDRESSED): the delete is conditional on
   106	    reconciled_at IS NULL — a SINGLE atomic statement that both checks and
   107	    acts. See chat_service._refund_predebit's docstring for the full
   108	    race-closure reasoning (identical here). Returns True if a refund was
   109	    actually issued, False if the row was already reconciled or already
   110	    removed by a prior settlement.
   111	    """
   112	    result = await db.execute(
   113	        sa.delete(CreditLedger)
   114	        .where(CreditLedger.id == ledger_id)
   115	        .where(CreditLedger.reconciled_at.is_(None))
   116	        .returning(CreditLedger.id)
   117	    )
   118	    refunded = result.scalar_one_or_none() is not None
   119	    if refunded:
   120	        await db.execute(
   121	            sa.update(User).where(User.id == user_id)
   122	            .values(credits_balance=User.credits_balance + pre_debited)
   123	        )
   124	    else:
   125	        logger.info(
   126	            "quote_billing.already_settled: ledger %s not refunded (already "
   127	            "reconciled or previously removed)", ledger_id,
   128	        )
   129	    await db.commit()
   130	    return refunded
   131	
   132	
   133	async def _settle_quote_search_predebit_after_failure(
   134	    *,
   135	    user_id: uuid.UUID,
   136	    pre_debited: int,
   137	    ledger_id: uuid.UUID,
   138	    use_independent_session: bool,
   139	    db: Optional[AsyncSession] = None,
   140	) -> bool:
   141	    """FIX3-A (Codex r3 #4, NOT ADDRESSED): the SOLE settlement resolver for
   142	    this endpoint's failure paths — CancelledError OR an ordinary exception
   143	    (e.g. db.commit() itself raising after the COMMIT actually landed on
   144	    the wire) — replacing FIX2-B(c)'s UsageRecord-marker existence check.
   145	
   146	    That marker check is superseded by FIX3-A(b)/(c)'s durable ledger
   147	    state: reconcile_credits() now ALWAYS stamps reconciled_at (including
   148	    the equal-cost no-op path) under a row lock, and _refund_predebit's
   149	    DELETE is now conditional on reconciled_at IS NULL — correct
   150	    regardless of whether the atomic commit has landed, is still landing,
   151	    or never will. There is nothing left for THIS function to "decide" —
   152	    it just calls _refund_predebit with the right session and surfaces
   153	    whether a refund actually happened.
   154	
   155	    use_independent_session=True (CancelledError): the request's own `db`
   156	    session may not be usable mid-cancellation — settle via a fresh
   157	    AsyncSessionLocal(), shielded from the cancellation being handled.
   158	    use_independent_session=False (ordinary exception): reuses the
   159	    request's own `db` (rolled back first by the caller) — matches the
   160	    existing pattern for non-cancellation failures.
   161	    """
   162	    if use_independent_session:
   163	        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as settle_db:
   164	            return await _refund_predebit(settle_db, user_id, pre_debited, ledger_id)
   165	    assert db is not None
   166	    return await _refund_predebit(db, user_id, pre_debited, ledger_id)
   167

   169	@router.post("/documents/{document_id}/quote-search", response_model=QuoteSearchResponse)
   170	async def create_quote_search(
   171	    document_id: uuid.UUID,
   172	    body: QuoteSearchRequest,
   173	    user: User = Depends(require_auth),
   174	    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db_session),
   175	):
   176	    if not await auth_chat_limiter.is_allowed(str(user.id)):
   177	        raise HTTPException(
   178	            status_code=429,
   179	            detail={"error": "RATE_LIMITED", "message": "Rate limit exceeded", "retry_after": 60},
   180	            headers={"Retry-After": "60"},
   181	        )
   182	
   183	    doc = await _verify_document(document_id, user, db)
   184	    if doc.status != "ready":
   185	        raise HTTPException(
   186	            status_code=409,
   187	            detail={"error": "DOCUMENT_NOT_READY", "message": "Document is not ready"},
   188	        )
   189	
   190	    # Billing (predebit through reconcile/refund below) is entirely OWNED by
   191	    # this endpoint too — quote_search_service.quote_search() does no
   192	    # credit_service calls of its own. It returns .usage/.model precisely so
   193	    # a caller can bill; it never bills itself.
   194	    balance = await credit_service.get_user_credits(db, user.id)
   195	    if balance < QUOTE_SEARCH_PREDEBIT_CREDITS:
   196	        raise HTTPException(
   197	            status_code=402,
   198	            detail={
   199	                "error": "INSUFFICIENT_CREDITS",
   200	                "message": "Insufficient credits to search for quotes",
   201	                "required": QUOTE_SEARCH_PREDEBIT_CREDITS,
   202	                "balance": balance,
   203	            },
   204	        )
   205	
   206	    ledger_id = await credit_service.debit_credits(
   207	        db,
   208	        user_id=user.id,
   209	        cost=QUOTE_SEARCH_PREDEBIT_CREDITS,
   210	        reason="quote_search",
   211	        ref_type="document",
   212	        ref_id=str(doc.id),
   213	    )
   214	    if ledger_id is None:
   215	        await db.rollback()
   216	        balance = await credit_service.get_user_credits(db, user.id)
   217	        raise HTTPException(
   218	            status_code=402,
   219	            detail={
   220	                "error": "INSUFFICIENT_CREDITS",
   221	                "message": "Insufficient credits to search for quotes",
   222	                "required": QUOTE_SEARCH_PREDEBIT_CREDITS,
   223	                "balance": balance,
   224	            },
   225	        )
   226	    await db.commit()
   227	
   228	    # FIX-4 (Codex r1 IMPORTANT #4): reconcile/usage/telemetry/commit are
   229	    # INSIDE this guarded region too, not just quote_search() — a failure
   230	    # ANYWHERE after predebit (including CancelledError, handled explicitly
   231	    # below) must refund it. The prior version's try/except wrapped only the
   232	    # quote_search() call, leaving a real 15-credit predebit permanently
   233	    # committed if reconcile/commit itself failed.
   234	    # Client-generated (not server_default) — no billing-correctness
   235	    # significance since FIX3-A (settlement now resolves via
   236	    # credit_ledger.reconciled_at, not a marker-row existence check), kept
   237	    # simply as a normal id assignment for the UsageRecord below.
   238	    usage_record_id = uuid.uuid4()
   239	    try:
   240	        result = await quote_search_service.quote_search(
   241	            db, document=doc, user=user, topic=body.topic, locale=body.locale or ""
   242	        )
   243	
   244	        prompt_tokens, completion_tokens = result.usage
   245	        actual_cost = credit_service.calculate_cost(prompt_tokens, completion_tokens, result.model, mode="balanced")

+# -------------------------- M3-B2: save-time re-verification --------------------------
+
+async def verify_saved_quote(
+    db: AsyncSession,
+    *,
+    document: Document,
+    chunk_id: uuid.UUID,
+    quote_text: str,
+    page_hint: Optional[int] = None,
+) -> Optional[QuoteCard]:
+    """M3-B2 (plan §8.1's fabrication-safety corollary): saving a quote must
+    NOT trust client-supplied tier/score/page/bboxes — a raw HTTP client
+    could otherwise persist arbitrary self-typed text as a "verified: exact"
+    card attributed to a real document, undermining the "no unverified text
+    reaches a rendered card" guarantee this system enforces everywhere else
+    (M1's verify_quote gate, M2's per-segment attribution). The save
+    endpoint therefore supplies ONLY what identifies WHAT to save (chunk_id,
+    quote_text); every trust field below is re-derived here through the
+    SAME verify_quote gate quote_search() uses, never taken from the caller.
+
+    `page_hint` disambiguates the rare case where the SAME exact wording
+    verifies on more than one page within a page_text chunk's range (a
+    repeated boilerplate clause — see _verify_against_segments's
+    docstring) — it only picks WHICH already-independently-verified
+    occurrence to persist, never fabricates one; an absent or non-matching
+    hint just falls back to the first verified occurrence.
+
+    Returns None when quote_text cannot be independently re-verified
+    against the chunk (chunk missing/reparsed since the original search,
+    belongs to a different document, or genuinely not a verbatim/
+    normalized/aligned match) — callers must reject the save.
+    """
+    chunk = await db.get(Chunk, chunk_id)
+    if chunk is None or chunk.document_id != document.id:
+        return None
+
+    neighbors = await _neighbor_chunks(db, chunk)
+    source = await build_quote_source(db, document.id, chunk, neighbors)
+    matches, _best_failure = _verify_against_segments(quote_text, source, document)
+
+    # `segment` is carried through alongside the attribution tuple so its
+    # own corpus text (the exact string verify_quote checked `quote_text`
+    # against) can be hashed below — the "verification corpus" §8.1 wants
+    # source_text_hash to represent (page text for page_text segments,
+    # chunk text for extracted_text segments).
+    attributed = [
+        (verification, segment, *_attribute_match(chunk, segment))
+        for verification, segment in matches
+        if not _is_ambiguous_multipage_extracted_segment(segment)
+    ]
+    if not attributed:
+        return None
+
+    verification, segment, page, page_end, bboxes, attributed_chunk_id = attributed[0]
+    if page_hint is not None:
+        for candidate in attributed:
+            if candidate[2] == page_hint:  # candidate = (verification, segment, page, ...)
+                verification, segment, page, page_end, bboxes, attributed_chunk_id = candidate
+                break
+
+    return QuoteCard(
+        display_text=verification.display_text or "",
+        page=page,
+        page_end=page_end,
+        bboxes=bboxes,
+        tier=verification.status,
+        source_kind=source.kind,
+        chunk_id=attributed_chunk_id,
+        score=verification.score,
+        # M3 review addition (plan §8.1 anchor fields): raw_start/raw_end
+        # are the verified slice's exact offsets WITHIN segment.text (see
+        # quote_verification_service.verify_quote — every verified tier
+        # returns them); source_text_hash lets a future revalidation pass
+        # detect whether that corpus has since changed.
+        source_text_hash=hashlib.sha256((segment.text or "").encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
+        quote_start=verification.raw_start,
+        quote_end=verification.raw_end,
+    )

   609	# -------------------------- M3-B2: save-time re-verification --------------------------
   610	
   611	async def verify_saved_quote(
   612	    db: AsyncSession,
   613	    *,
   614	    document: Document,
   615	    chunk_id: uuid.UUID,
   616	    quote_text: str,
   617	    page_hint: Optional[int] = None,
   618	) -> Optional[QuoteCard]:
   619	    """M3-B2 (plan §8.1's fabrication-safety corollary): saving a quote must
   620	    NOT trust client-supplied tier/score/page/bboxes — a raw HTTP client
   621	    could otherwise persist arbitrary self-typed text as a "verified: exact"
   622	    card attributed to a real document, undermining the "no unverified text
   623	    reaches a rendered card" guarantee this system enforces everywhere else
   624	    (M1's verify_quote gate, M2's per-segment attribution). The save
   625	    endpoint therefore supplies ONLY what identifies WHAT to save (chunk_id,
   626	    quote_text); every trust field below is re-derived here through the
   627	    SAME verify_quote gate quote_search() uses, never taken from the caller.
   628	
   629	    `page_hint` disambiguates the rare case where the SAME exact wording
   630	    verifies on more than one page within a page_text chunk's range (a
   631	    repeated boilerplate clause — see _verify_against_segments's
   632	    docstring) — it only picks WHICH already-independently-verified
   633	    occurrence to persist, never fabricates one; an absent or non-matching
   634	    hint just falls back to the first verified occurrence.
   635	
   636	    Returns None when quote_text cannot be independently re-verified
   637	    against the chunk (chunk missing/reparsed since the original search,
   638	    belongs to a different document, or genuinely not a verbatim/
   639	    normalized/aligned match) — callers must reject the save.
   640	    """
   641	    chunk = await db.get(Chunk, chunk_id)
   642	    if chunk is None or chunk.document_id != document.id:
   643	        return None
   644	
   645	    neighbors = await _neighbor_chunks(db, chunk)
   646	    source = await build_quote_source(db, document.id, chunk, neighbors)
   647	    matches, _best_failure = _verify_against_segments(quote_text, source, document)
   648	
   649	    # `segment` is carried through alongside the attribution tuple so its
   650	    # own corpus text (the exact string verify_quote checked `quote_text`
   651	    # against) can be hashed below — the "verification corpus" §8.1 wants
   652	    # source_text_hash to represent (page text for page_text segments,
   653	    # chunk text for extracted_text segments).
   654	    attributed = [
   655	        (verification, segment, *_attribute_match(chunk, segment))
   656	        for verification, segment in matches
   657	        if not _is_ambiguous_multipage_extracted_segment(segment)
   658	    ]
   659	    if not attributed:
   660	        return None
   661	
   662	    verification, segment, page, page_end, bboxes, attributed_chunk_id = attributed[0]
   663	    if page_hint is not None:
   664	        for candidate in attributed:
   665	            if candidate[2] == page_hint:  # candidate = (verification, segment, page, ...)
   666	                verification, segment, page, page_end, bboxes, attributed_chunk_id = candidate
   667	                break
   668	
   669	    return QuoteCard(
   670	        display_text=verification.display_text or "",
   671	        page=page,
   672	        page_end=page_end,
   673	        bboxes=bboxes,
   674	        tier=verification.status,
   675	        source_kind=source.kind,
   676	        chunk_id=attributed_chunk_id,
   677	        score=verification.score,
   678	        # M3 review addition (plan §8.1 anchor fields): raw_start/raw_end
   679	        # are the verified slice's exact offsets WITHIN segment.text (see
   680	        # quote_verification_service.verify_quote — every verified tier
   681	        # returns them); source_text_hash lets a future revalidation pass
   682	        # detect whether that corpus has since changed.
   683	        source_text_hash=hashlib.sha256((segment.text or "").encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
   684	        quote_start=verification.raw_start,
   685	        quote_end=verification.raw_end,
   686	    )

   149	def _json_from_text(text: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
   150	    content = (text or "").strip()
   151	    if content.startswith("```"):
   152	        content = _FENCE_RE.sub("", content)
   153	    try:
   154	        data = json.loads(content)
   155	    except json.JSONDecodeError:
   156	        match = re.search(r"\{.*\}", content, flags=re.DOTALL)
   157	        if not match:
   158	            raise
   159	        data = json.loads(match.group(0))

   161	        raise ValueError("quote search response must be a JSON object")
   162	    return data

   165	# -------------------------- candidate gathering --------------------------
   166	
   167	async def _all_document_chunks(db: AsyncSession, document_id: uuid.UUID) -> list[Chunk]:
   168	    result = await db.execute(
   169	        select(Chunk).where(Chunk.document_id == document_id).order_by(Chunk.chunk_index)
   170	    )
   171	    return list(result.scalars().all())
   172	
   173	
   174	async def _all_document_pages(db: AsyncSession, document_id: uuid.UUID) -> list[Page]:
   175	    result = await db.execute(
   176	        select(Page).where(Page.document_id == document_id).order_by(Page.page_number)
   177	    )
   178	    return list(result.scalars().all())

   180	
   181	def _term_scan_candidates(chunks: list[Chunk], pages: list[Page], topic: str) -> list[Chunk]:
   182	    """Deterministic candidate expansion (§8.3/§8.1): normalized phrase/term
   183	    scan over the document's chunks (and page text where present), merged
   184	    into retrieval candidates before generation. Over-retrieve alone is
   185	    insufficient recall for verbatim quote finding (24-chunk cap,
   186	    lexical=ILIKE only) — a short exact phrase can miss embedding-similarity
   187	    retrieval entirely while still being locatable by a literal (normalized)
   188	    scan.
   189	
   190	    FIX-6 (Codex r1 IMPORTANT #6): two corrections found in review —
   191	    (1) fuzzy=True (casefold) so a differently-cased topic still matches
   192	    ("Climate Risk" vs. a chunk containing "climate risk"); tier selection at
   193	    verify time is unaffected — this only widens which chunks reach the LLM
   194	    proposal step. (2) scans Page.content, not just Chunk.text — B1's
   195	    page-text corpus can hold a phrase whole where chunking split it
   196	    differently across chunk boundaries; a page-content match surfaces via
   197	    every chunk that overlaps that page (so the LLM still gets numbered
   198	    chunk excerpts, never raw page text)."""
   199	    norm_topic, _ = normalize(topic, fuzzy=True)
   200	    norm_topic = norm_topic.strip()
   201	    if not norm_topic:
   202	        return []
   203	    terms = [t for t in norm_topic.split(" ") if len(t) >= MIN_TERM_LEN]
   204	    if not terms:
   205	        return []
   206	
   207	    def _matches(text: str) -> bool:
   208	        norm_text, _ = normalize(text or "", fuzzy=True)
   209	        if not norm_text:
   210	            return False
   211	        return norm_topic in norm_text or any(t in norm_text for t in terms)
   212	
   213	    hits: list[Chunk] = []
   214	    seen: set[uuid.UUID] = set()
   215	    for ch in chunks:
   216	        if _matches(ch.text):
   217	            hits.append(ch)
   218	            seen.add(ch.id)
   219	
   220	    if pages:
   221	        matched_pages = {p.page_number for p in pages if p.content and _matches(p.content)}
   222	        if matched_pages:
   223	            for ch in chunks:
   224	                if ch.id in seen:
   225	                    continue
   226	                if any(ch.page_start <= pn <= ch.page_end for pn in matched_pages):
   227	                    hits.append(ch)
   228	                    seen.add(ch.id)
   229	
   230	    return hits
   231	
   232	
   233	async def _fetch_chunks_by_id(db: AsyncSession, chunk_ids: list[uuid.UUID]) -> dict[uuid.UUID, Chunk]:
   234	    if not chunk_ids:
   235	        return {}
   236	    result = await db.execute(select(Chunk).where(Chunk.id.in_(chunk_ids)))
   237	    return {c.id: c for c in result.scalars().all()}
   238	
   239	
   240	async def _build_candidates(
   241	    db: AsyncSession, document: Document, topic: str
   242	) -> tuple[list[Chunk], int]:
   243	    """Retrieval (existing hybrid stack, ~2x chat top_k) + deterministic
   244	    normalized term/phrase scan, merged and deduped (relevance-ranked
   245	    retrieval first, then term-scan-only hits), capped at
   246	    MAX_CANDIDATE_CHUNKS. Returns (candidates, scanned_chunks) —
   247	    scanned_chunks is the document's total chunk count examined by the term
   248	    scan (§8.3 telemetry / empty-result UX: "show count + what was scanned")."""
   249	    all_chunks = await _all_document_chunks(db, document.id)
   250	    all_pages = await _all_document_pages(db, document.id)
   251	
   252	    route = _query_router.route(topic, is_collection=False)
   253	    retrieval = await corrective_retrieval_service.retrieve_single(
   254	        topic, route, document.id, top_k=RETRIEVAL_TOP_K, db=db, doc_pages=document.page_count,
   255	    )
   256	    retrieved_ids = [item["chunk_id"] for item in retrieval.retrieved if item.get("chunk_id")]
   257	    retrieved_map = await _fetch_chunks_by_id(db, retrieved_ids)
   258	
   259	    term_hits = _term_scan_candidates(all_chunks, all_pages, topic)
   260	
   261	    ordered: list[Chunk] = []
   262	    seen: set[uuid.UUID] = set()
   263	    for cid in retrieved_ids:
   264	        ch = retrieved_map.get(cid)
   265	        if ch is None or ch.id in seen:
   266	            continue
   267	        seen.add(ch.id)
   268	        ordered.append(ch)
   269	    for ch in term_hits:
   270	        if ch.id in seen:
   271	            continue
   272	        seen.add(ch.id)
   273	        ordered.append(ch)
   274	
   275	    return ordered[:MAX_CANDIDATE_CHUNKS], len(all_chunks)
   276	
   277	
   278	def _candidate_pages_count(candidates: list[Chunk]) -> int:
   279	    """FIX-6 telemetry: distinct pages spanned by the final candidate set
   280	    (union of each candidate chunk's own page_start..page_end range) —
   281	    "how much of the document did the search actually look at," independent
   282	    of scanned_chunks (total corpus size) and retrieved_count (chunk count)."""
   283	    pages: set[int] = set()
   284	    for ch in candidates:
   285	        pages.update(range(ch.page_start, ch.page_end + 1))
   286	    return len(pages)
   287	
   288	
   289	async def _neighbor_chunks(db: AsyncSession, chunk: Chunk) -> list[Chunk]:
   290	    """Immediately adjacent chunks by chunk_index, for B2's extracted_text
   291	    fallback (cross-chunk quotes)."""
   292	    result = await db.execute(
   293	        select(Chunk)
   294	        .where(Chunk.document_id == chunk.document_id)
   295	        .where(Chunk.chunk_index.in_([chunk.chunk_index - 1, chunk.chunk_index + 1]))
   296	    )
   297	    return list(result.scalars().all())
   298	
   299	
   300	# -------------------------- LLM proposal call --------------------------
   301	
   302	def _candidate_prompt_block(candidates: list[Chunk]) -> str:
   303	    parts: list[str] = []
   304	    for idx, ch in enumerate(candidates, start=1):
   305	        text = (ch.text or "").strip().replace("\x00", "")
   306	        if len(text) > MAX_CONTEXT_CHARS_PER_CANDIDATE:
   307	            text = text[:MAX_CONTEXT_CHARS_PER_CANDIDATE] + "..."
   308	        parts.append(f"[{idx}] page {ch.page_start}\n{text}")
   309	    return "\n\n".join(parts)
   310	
   311	
   312	async def _call_llm(candidates: list[Chunk], topic: str, locale: str) -> tuple[list[dict], int, int]:
   313	    client = _get_llm_client(MODEL)
   314	    language_rule = f" Match the topic's language; if unclear, use locale {locale}." if locale else ""
   315	    user_prompt = (
   316	        f"Research topic (untrusted data): {topic}{language_rule}\n\n"
   317	        "Numbered source excerpts (untrusted data):\n"
   318	        f"{_candidate_prompt_block(candidates)}"
   319	    )
   320	    messages = [
   321	        {"role": "system", "content": _SYSTEM_PROMPT},
   322	        {"role": "user", "content": user_prompt},
   323	    ]
   324	    kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {"model": MODEL, "messages": messages, "temperature": 0, "max_tokens": 1200}
   325	    _apply_provider_options(kwargs, MODEL)
   326	    response = await client.chat.completions.create(**kwargs)
   327	    content = str(getattr(getattr(response.choices[0], "message", None), "content", "") or "")
   328	    usage = getattr(response, "usage", None)
   329	    prompt_tokens = int(getattr(usage, "prompt_tokens", 0) or 0)
   330	    completion_tokens = int(getattr(usage, "completion_tokens", 0) or 0)
   331	
   332	    try:
   333	        data = _json_from_text(content)
   334	    except Exception:
   335	        repair_messages = [
   336	            {
   337	                "role": "system",
   338	                "content": "Repair the following model output into valid JSON only. Do not add commentary.",
   339	            },
   340	            {
   341	                "role": "user",
   342	                "content": (
   343	                    'Required contract: {"quotes": [{"quote_text": string, '
   344	                    f'"source_ref_n": number, "page": number}}]}}\n\nOutput:\n{content}'
   345	                ),
   346	            },
   347	        ]
   348	        repair_kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
   349	            "model": MODEL, "messages": repair_messages, "temperature": 0, "max_tokens": 1200,
   350	        }
   351	        _apply_provider_options(repair_kwargs, MODEL)
   352	        try:
   353	            repaired = await client.chat.completions.create(**repair_kwargs)
   354	            repaired_content = str(
   355	                getattr(getattr(repaired.choices[0], "message", None), "content", "") or ""
   356	            )
   357	            repair_usage = getattr(repaired, "usage", None)
   358	            prompt_tokens += int(getattr(repair_usage, "prompt_tokens", 0) or 0)
   359	            completion_tokens += int(getattr(repair_usage, "completion_tokens", 0) or 0)
   360	            data = _json_from_text(repaired_content)
   361	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — malformed output degrades to "no quotes", never crashes
   362	            logger.warning("quote_search LLM output unrecoverable after repair retry: %s", exc)
   363	            data = {}
   364	
   365	    quotes = data.get("quotes")
   366	    if not isinstance(quotes, list):
   367	        quotes = []
   368	    return quotes, prompt_tokens, completion_tokens
   369	
   370	
   371	# -------------------------- disposition --------------------------
   372	
   373	def _valid_bbox(bb: Any) -> bool:
   374	    return isinstance(bb, dict) and all(isinstance(bb.get(k), (int, float)) for k in ("x", "y", "w", "h"))
   375	
   376	
   377	def _majority_bbox_page(bboxes_list: list[dict], fallback_page: int) -> tuple[int, list[dict]]:
   378	    """Mirror extraction_service._citation_from_chunk's best_page derivation,
   379	    scoped to a SINGLE chunk's own bboxes (never a multi-chunk/multi-page
   380	    pool): whichever page most of THIS chunk's bboxes actually sit on (a
   381	    chunk can span pages; bboxes are ground truth, page_start is not
   382	    necessarily where the matched text is)."""
   383	    bboxes = [bb for bb in (bboxes_list or []) if _valid_bbox(bb)]
   384	    if not bboxes:
   385	        return fallback_page, []
   386	    page_counts: dict[int, int] = {}
   387	    for bb in bboxes:
   388	        raw_page = bb.get("page", fallback_page)
   389	        page = int(raw_page) if isinstance(raw_page, (int, float)) else fallback_page
   390	        page_counts[page] = page_counts.get(page, 0) + 1
   391	    best_page = min(page_counts, key=lambda p: (-page_counts[p], p))
   392	    page_bboxes = [bb for bb in bboxes if int(bb.get("page", fallback_page)) == best_page]
   393	    return best_page, page_bboxes
   394	
   395	
   396	def _is_ambiguous_multipage_extracted_segment(matched_segment: QuoteSourceSegment) -> bool:
   397	    """FIX2-A(a) (Codex r2 #2, NOT ADDRESSED): an extracted_text segment is
   398	    exactly one CHUNK, and a chunk can itself span multiple pages
   399	    (page_start != page_end). Verification ran against that chunk's WHOLE
   400	    text as one blob, so there is no way to know which of its pages the
   401	    matched slice actually sits on — majority-vote bbox counting over the
   402	    segment's entire bbox pool doesn't answer that (Codex's exact probe: a
   403	    p1-2 segment, quote physically in the page-1 portion, bboxes 1xp1+2xp2 —
   404	    majority vote picks p2, which is wrong). Single-page segments
   405	    (page_start == page_end) have no such ambiguity and are unaffected.
   406	    """
   407	    return matched_segment.chunk_id is not None and matched_segment.page_start != matched_segment.page_end
   408	
   409	
   410	def _attribute_match(
   411	    chunk: Chunk, matched_segment: QuoteSourceSegment
   412	) -> tuple[int, int, list[dict], str]:
   413	    """FIX-2 (Codex r1 BLOCKER #2 — page attribution from the verified
   414	    slice): page/page_end/bboxes/chunk_id ALWAYS come from the segment that
   415	    actually verified, never a majority-vote guess spanning the whole
   416	    candidate chunk's (or its whole multi-page range's) bbox distribution.
   417	
   418	    page_text segments are exactly one page each (no ambiguity at all) —
   419	    bboxes are the ORIGINALLY CITED chunk's own bboxes (pages don't carry
   420	    bbox metadata), filtered to that exact verified page.
   421	
   422	    extracted_text segments are exactly one chunk each (the cited chunk, or
   423	    one neighbor). Callers MUST have already rejected ambiguous multi-page
   424	    segments via `_is_ambiguous_multipage_extracted_segment` before calling
   425	    this — by the time we get here, `matched_segment.page_start ==
   426	    matched_segment.page_end`, so majority-vote bbox filtering is just
   427	    "this segment's own bboxes on its own single page," not a genuine guess.
   428	    chunk_id follows the match, not the LLM's cited ref, since that's
   429	    genuinely where the text lives.
   430	    """

   488	@router.post("/documents/{document_id}/quotes", response_model=SavedQuoteResponse, status_code=201)
   489	async def create_saved_quote(

   496	    """Save a quote card. `chunk_id`/`quote_text` (+ optional `page_hint`)
   497	    identify WHAT to save — every trust field actually persisted (tier,
   498	    score, page, page_end, bboxes, source_kind) is RE-DERIVED server-side
   499	    via quote_search_service.verify_saved_quote(), never taken from the
   500	    request body. A client cannot forge a "verified" card by supplying
   501	    arbitrary tier/score/page values directly; see that function's
   502	    docstring for the full rationale."""

    24	def sanitize_filename(name: str, max_length: int = 200) -> str:
    25	    """Sanitize filename to prevent path traversal and special character issues."""
    26	    name = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", name)
    27	    name = os.path.basename(name)
    28	    name = re.sub(r'[\x00-\x1f\x7f]', '', name)
    29	    name = re.sub(r'[<>:"|?*\\]', '_', name)

    33	        base = parts[0].replace('.', '_')
    34	        name = f"{base}.{parts[1]}"
    35	    if len(name) > max_length:
    36	        base, ext = os.path.splitext(name)
    37	        name = base[:max_length - len(ext)] + ext

   447	
   448	def _dedup_signature(source_kind: str, verification: Any) -> str:
   449	    """§8.1 dedup key component distinguishing two genuinely different quote
   450	    occurrences that happen to share normalized text + page range.
   451	
   452	    Stable for kind="page_text": raw offsets are relative to the SAME
   453	    per-document page-text corpus regardless of which candidate chunk led to
   454	    the match, so two independent occurrences on one page get distinct
   455	    signatures while the identical occurrence (found twice via different
   456	    routes) collapses.
   457	
   458	    Omitted for kind="extracted_text": the verification corpus differs per
   459	    originating chunk (chunk ± neighbours), so raw offsets aren't globally
   460	    comparable across candidates — and chunk overlap means the SAME real
   461	    occurrence, independently located via two overlapping candidate chunks,
   462	    must still collapse to one card (a known, accepted simplification: a
   463	    genuinely repeated short phrase within one page of a chunk-fallback doc
   464	    could theoretically over-collapse; not observed as a practical risk for
   465	    quote-finding and flagged here for Codex review)."""
   466	    if source_kind == "page_text":
   467	        return f"{verification.raw_start}-{verification.raw_end}"
   468	    return ""
   469	
   470	
   471	def _verify_against_segments(
   472	    quote_text: str, source: QuoteSource, document: Document,
   473	) -> tuple[list[tuple[Any, QuoteSourceSegment]], Any]:
   474	    """FIX-2 (Codex r1 BLOCKER #2): verify against EACH segment separately —
   475	    never a concatenated multi-page/multi-chunk blob.
   476	
   477	    Returns (matches, best_failure).
   478	
   479	    FIX2-A(b) (Codex r2 #2, NOT ADDRESSED): `matches` holds EVERY verifying
   480	    segment for kind="page_text" — the prior "first segment wins" behavior
   481	    silently dropped genuine duplicate occurrences of the SAME exact wording
   482	    appearing on more than one page within the cited chunk's own page range
   483	    (e.g. a boilerplate clause repeated verbatim). Each is independently
   484	    verified and gets its own card (§8.1 dedup already distinguishes them by
   485	    page, so real duplicates never collapse into one and never over-count).
   486	
   487	    For kind="extracted_text", `matches` holds AT MOST ONE entry — the FIRST
   488	    segment that verifies (segments are ordered cited-chunk-then-neighbors,
   489	    so the cited chunk is always tried before a neighbor). This preserves
   490	    the existing, deliberate simplification that chunk overlap meaning the
   491	    SAME occurrence located via two different chunks collapses to one card
   492	    (see `_dedup_signature`'s docstring) — extracted_text does NOT get the
   493	    multi-match treatment page_text does.
   494	
   495	    `best_failure` is the highest-scoring verify_quote() failure across ALL
   496	    segments tried, for a discard reason when `matches` is empty.
   497	    """
   498	    matches: list[tuple[Any, QuoteSourceSegment]] = []
   499	    best_failure: Any = None
   500	    for segment in source.segments:
   501	        v = verify_quote(
   502	            quote_text, segment.text,
   503	            text_quality=document.text_quality, parse_method=document.parse_method,
   504	        )
   505	        if v.verified:
   506	            matches.append((v, segment))
   507	            if source.kind != "page_text":
   508	                break
   509	            continue
   510	        if best_failure is None or v.score > best_failure.score:
   511	            best_failure = v
   512	    return matches, best_failure

codex
Backend trust and ownership checks are structurally sound so far: chunk/document binding is enforced before verification, `page_hint` can only select an already-verified occurrence, and PATCH/DELETE predicate on both quote ID and user ID. One cap path is now under active challenge: the count and insert are in separate transactions with no per-user serialization, so I’m determining whether concurrent distinct saves can exceed 30 despite the existing idempotency race handling.
