    15	  onJump: (quote: SavedQuote, index: number) => void;
    16	  onDeleted: (quoteId: string) => void;
    17	}
    18	
    19	/**
    20	 * A single saved quote in the Evidence Board (M3-F2). Renders the STORED
    21	 * snapshot fields (tier/score/source_kind taken at save time) — never
    22	 * re-verifies, matching the backend's own no-reverification-on-read
    23	 * guarantee (wave-m3-backend-report.md's B3 section): a saved quote's
    24	 * trust labels can go visually stale relative to a since-reparsed
    25	 * document, which is intentional v1 scope, not a bug.
    26	 */
    27	export default function SavedQuoteCard({ quote, index, biblio, onJump, onDeleted }: SavedQuoteCardProps) {
    28	  const { tOr } = useLocale();
    29	  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
    30	  const [note, setNote] = useState(quote.note || '');
    31	  const [savingNote, setSavingNote] = useState(false);
    32	  const [noteError, setNoteError] = useState(false);
    33	  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false);
    34	
    35	  // The quote object is the parent's source of truth; re-sync the local
    36	  // draft if it changes out from under us (e.g. a PATCH from elsewhere).
    37	  useEffect(() => {
    38	    setNote(quote.note || '');
    39	  }, [quote.id, quote.note]);
    40	
    41	  const handleCopy = async () => {
    42	    const apaInText = formatApaInText(biblio, quote.page);
    43	    const text = `"${quote.quoteText}" ${apaInText}`;
    44	    try {
    45	      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    46	      setCopied(true);
    47	      window.setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    48	    } catch {
    49	      // Best-effort, no toast — matches QuoteResultCard's copy handling.
    50	    }
    51	  };
    52	
    53	  const handleNoteBlur = async () => {
    54	    const trimmed = note.trim();
    55	    const original = quote.note || '';
    56	    if (trimmed === original) return; // no-op PATCH avoidance
    57	    setSavingNote(true);
    58	    setNoteError(false);
    59	    try {
    60	      await updateSavedQuoteNote(quote.id, trimmed || null);
    61	    } catch {
    62	      // Revert to last-known-good so the textarea doesn't silently claim a
    63	      // save that didn't happen.
    64	      setNote(original);
    65	      setNoteError(true);
    66	    } finally {
    67	      setSavingNote(false);
    68	    }
    69	  };
    70	
    71	  const handleDelete = async () => {
    72	    if (deleting) return;
    73	    setDeleting(true);
    74	    try {
    75	      await deleteSavedQuote(quote.id);
    76	      onDeleted(quote.id);
    77	    } catch (err) {
    78	      // 404 SAVED_QUOTE_NOT_FOUND means it's already gone (e.g. deleted
    79	      // from another tab) — treat that as success rather than leaving a
    80	      // ghost row the user can't get rid of.
    81	      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404) {
    82	        onDeleted(quote.id);
    83	        return;
    84	      }
    85	      setDeleting(false);
    86	    }
    87	  };
    88	
    89	  return (
    90	    <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--reader-border)] bg-[var(--reader-panel-solid)] px-4 py-3">
    91	      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
    92	        <span className="rounded-full bg-zinc-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300">
    93	          {tierLabel(quote.tier, tOr)}
    94	        </span>
    95	        <span
    96	          className={
    97	            quote.sourceKind === 'page_text'
    98	              ? 'text-[11px] text-[var(--reader-muted)]'
    99	              : 'inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-amber-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-950/30 dark:text-amber-200'
   100	          }
   101	        >
   102	          {trustLabel(quote.sourceKind, tOr)}
   103	        </span>
   104	      </div>
   105	
   106	      <blockquote className="mt-2 border-l-2 border-blue-600 pl-3 text-sm italic leading-relaxed text-[var(--reader-ink)] dark:border-blue-400">
   107	        &ldquo;{quote.quoteText}&rdquo;
   108	      </blockquote>
   109	
   110	      <div className="mt-2">
   111	        <textarea
   112	          value={note}
   113	          onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}
   114	          onBlur={() => void handleNoteBlur()}
   115	          maxLength={2000}
   116	          rows={2}
   117	          placeholder={tOr('quoteFinder.notePlaceholder', 'Add a note...')}
   118	          aria-label={tOr('quoteFinder.notePlaceholder', 'Add a note...')}
   119	          className="w-full resize-y rounded-md border border-[var(--reader-border)] bg-[var(--reader-panel-solid)] px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-[var(--reader-ink)] outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-500"
   120	        />
   121	        {savingNote ? (
   122	          <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-[var(--reader-muted)]">{tOr('quoteFinder.noteSaving', 'Saving note...')}</p>
   123	        ) : noteError ? (
   124	          <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-red-700 dark:text-red-300">{tOr('quoteFinder.noteSaveError', 'Failed to save note')}</p>
   125	        ) : null}
   126	      </div>
   127	
   128	      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
   129	        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-[var(--reader-muted)]">
   130	          <MapPin size={12} aria-hidden="true" />
   131	          <span>{pageRangeLabel(quote, tOr)}</span>
   132	        </div>
   133	        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   134	          <button
   135	            type="button"
   136	            onClick={() => onJump(quote, index)}
   137	            className="inline-flex min-h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-[var(--reader-evidence-border)] bg-[var(--reader-evidence-soft)] px-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-[var(--reader-evidence)] transition-colors hover:brightness-95 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-500"
   138	          >
   139	            <MapPin size={12} aria-hidden="true" />
   140	            {tOr('quoteFinder.jump', 'Jump to page')}
   141	          </button>
   142	          <button
   143	            type="button"
   144	            onClick={() => void handleCopy()}
   145	            className="inline-flex min-h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-[var(--reader-border)] bg-[var(--reader-panel-solid)] px-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-[var(--reader-ink)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--reader-panel-muted)] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-500"
   146	          >
   147	            {copied ? <Check size={12} className="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" aria-hidden="true" /> : <Copy size={12} aria-hidden="true" />}
   148	            {copied ? tOr('quoteFinder.copied', 'Copied') : tOr('quoteFinder.copy', 'Copy quote + citation')}
   149	          </button>
   150	          <button
   151	            type="button"
   152	            onClick={() => void handleDelete()}
   153	            disabled={deleting}
   154	            aria-label={tOr('quoteFinder.delete', 'Delete')}
   155	            title={tOr('quoteFinder.delete', 'Delete')}
   156	            className="inline-flex min-h-8 min-w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-[var(--reader-border)] bg-[var(--reader-panel-solid)] text-[var(--reader-muted)] transition-colors hover:border-red-200 hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-700 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-500 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 dark:hover:border-red-900/50 dark:hover:bg-red-950/30 dark:hover:text-red-300"
   157	          >
   158	            {deleting ? <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none" aria-hidden="true" /> : <Trash2 size={14} aria-hidden="true" />}
   159	          </button>
   160	        </div>
   161	      </div>
   162	    </div>
   163	  );
   164	}

     1	import type { QuoteCard, SavedQuote } from '../../lib/api';
     2	import type { Citation } from '../../types';
     3	
     4	type TOrFn = (key: string, fallback: string, params?: Record<string, string | number>) => string;
     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * Builds the same `Citation` shape the rest of the app uses for
     8	 * `navigateToCitation`/`onCitationClick` (store/index.ts's citation-jump
     9	 * action) from a verified quote card, so Jump behaves identically whether
    10	 * the card came from the Quote Finder panel (F1) or a chat quote-card
    11	 * artifact (F3). `card.bboxes` are the CITED CHUNK's bboxes for the
    12	 * quote's page (§8.2 v1 decision) — highlight location is approximate,
    13	 * not the exact quote span; see `approxHighlightLabel` below.
    14	 */
    15	export function citationFromQuoteCard(card: QuoteCard, documentId: string, index: number): Citation {
    16	  return {
    17	    refIndex: card.refIndex ?? index + 1,
    18	    chunkId: card.chunkId,
    19	    page: card.page,
    20	    pageEnd: card.pageEnd,
    21	    bboxes: card.bboxes || [],
    22	    textSnippet: card.displayText,
    23	    focusSnippet: card.displayText,
    24	    offset: 0,
    25	    documentId,
    26	  };
    27	}
    28	
    29	/**
    30	 * Same `Citation` shape as `citationFromQuoteCard`, built from a SAVED
    31	 * quote instead (M3-F2, Evidence Board). `SavedQuoteResponse` never
    32	 * exposes `source_chunk_id` — by design, it's nullable server-side (a
    33	 * reparse SET NULLs it rather than cascading the delete, precisely so the
    34	 * saved row survives) — so there's no real chunk id to carry here.
    35	 * `navigateToCitation` never reads `chunkId` for anything functional (only
    36	 * page/bboxes/textSnippet/focusSnippet), so the saved quote's own id is a
    37	 * harmless, unique stand-in rather than an empty string.
    38	 */
    39	export function citationFromSavedQuote(quote: SavedQuote, index: number): Citation {
    40	  return {
    41	    refIndex: index + 1,
    42	    chunkId: quote.id,
    43	    page: quote.page,
    44	    pageEnd: quote.pageEnd,
    45	    bboxes: quote.bboxes || [],
    46	    textSnippet: quote.quoteText,
    47	    focusSnippet: quote.quoteText,
    48	    offset: 0,
    49	    documentId: quote.documentId,
    50	  };
    51	}
    52	
    53	export function tierLabel(tier: string, tOr: TOrFn): string {
    54	  if (tier === 'exact') return tOr('quoteFinder.tier.exact', 'Exact match');
    55	  if (tier === 'normalized') return tOr('quoteFinder.tier.normalized', 'Normalized match');
    56	  if (tier === 'aligned') return tOr('quoteFinder.tier.aligned', 'Close match');
    57	  return tier;
    58	}
    59	
    60	/** Honest trust label (plan §8.1, hardened per Codex M2 r1 BLOCKER #1):
    61	 * page-text-verified quotes say "verified against page text" — no caveat
    62	 * needed. Extracted-text (chunk-fallback) quotes carry an EXPLICIT caveat
    63	 * instead of a plain "verified against extracted text" label: the parser's
    64	 * hard-hyphen handling can silently drop a line-break hyphen, so a
    65	 * chunk-fallback "exact" match is not guaranteed byte-identical to the
    66	 * source the way a page-text match is. The caveat text IS the label —
    67	 * never render the unqualified claim for this kind. */
    68	export function trustLabel(sourceKind: string, tOr: TOrFn): string {
    69	  return sourceKind === 'page_text'
    70	    ? tOr('quoteFinder.trust.pageText', 'Verified against page text')
    71	    : tOr('quoteFinder.trust.extractedCaveat', 'Verified against extracted text — line-break hyphenation may be normalized.');
    72	}
    73	
    74	/**
    75	 * Headline claim shown once a result set exists (Codex M2 r1 BLOCKER #1
    76	 * fix). The panel/artifact used to claim "word-for-word quotes from this
    77	 * document" unconditionally, which is only true for page_text-kind
    78	 * results — extracted_text-kind results can have a mutated hard-hyphen
    79	 * (see `trustLabel` above). Per-card labels still govern each card; this
    80	 * headline is the WEAKEST kind present across the whole set, so a mixed
    81	 * result never overstates confidence for the set as a whole.
    82	 */
    83	export function resultKindHeadline(cards: QuoteCard[], tOr: TOrFn): string {
    84	  const allPageText = cards.length > 0 && cards.every((c) => c.sourceKind === 'page_text');
    85	  return allPageText
    86	    ? tOr('quoteFinder.trust.pageTextHeadline', "Word-for-word quotes, verified against this document's page text.")
    87	    : tOr('quoteFinder.trust.extractedCaveat', 'Verified against extracted text — line-break hyphenation may be normalized.');
    88	}
    89	
    90	export function approxHighlightLabel(tOr: TOrFn): string {
    91	  return tOr('quoteFinder.approxHighlight', 'Highlight location is approximate');
    92	}
    93	
    94	/** Structurally typed to `{page, pageEnd}` (not the full `QuoteCard`) so it
    95	 * works for both a live search result and a saved quote without either
    96	 * type needing to know about the other. */
    97	export function pageRangeLabel(card: { page: number; pageEnd: number }, tOr: TOrFn): string {
    98	  if (card.pageEnd && card.pageEnd !== card.page) {
    99	    return tOr('quoteFinder.pageRange', 'p. {start}–{end}', { start: card.page, end: card.pageEnd });
   100	  }
   101	  return tOr('quoteFinder.page', 'p. {page}', { page: card.page });
   102	}

     3	import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
     4	import type { DocumentBiblioCsl, SavedQuote } from '../../lib/api';
     5	import { getDocumentBiblio } from '../../lib/api';
     6	import SavedQuoteCard from './SavedQuoteCard';
     7	
     8	interface SavedQuoteListProps {
     9	  documentId: string;
    10	  quotes: SavedQuote[];
    11	  onJump: (quote: SavedQuote, index: number) => void;
    12	  onDeleted: (quoteId: string) => void;
    13	  /** Localized "n of 30 saved" cap indicator (M3-F3), rendered above the
    14	   * list. Undefined on paid plans, which don't get the indicator. */
    15	  capLine?: string;
    16	}
    17	
    18	/**
    19	 * Per-document Evidence Board list (M3-F2) — the "Saved" tab/section
    20	 * inside the Quote Finder panel. Fetches this document's biblio once,
    21	 * same pattern as QuoteCardList, so every card's Copy action can append
    22	 * an APA in-text citation without a per-card round trip.
    23	 */
    24	export default function SavedQuoteList({ documentId, quotes, onJump, onDeleted, capLine }: SavedQuoteListProps) {
    25	  const [biblio, setBiblio] = useState<DocumentBiblioCsl | null>(null);
    26	
    27	  useEffect(() => {
    28	    let cancelled = false;
    29	    setBiblio(null);
    30	    getDocumentBiblio(documentId)
    31	      .then((res) => {
    32	        if (!cancelled) setBiblio(res.cslJson);
    33	      })
    34	      .catch(() => {
    35	        if (!cancelled) setBiblio({});
    36	      });
    37	    return () => {
    38	      cancelled = true;
    39	    };
    40	  }, [documentId]);
    41	
    42	  return (
    43	    <div>
    44	      {capLine ? (
    45	        <p className="mb-2 px-1 text-xs text-[var(--reader-muted)]">{capLine}</p>
    46	      ) : null}
    47	      <div className="space-y-3">
    48	        {quotes.map((quote, index) => (
    49	          <SavedQuoteCard
    50	            key={quote.id}
    51	            quote={quote}
    52	            index={index}
    53	            biblio={biblio}
    54	            onJump={onJump}
    55	            onDeleted={onDeleted}
    56	          />
    57	        ))}
    58	      </div>
    59	    </div>
    60	  );
    61	}

     3	import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

     5	import { useLocale } from '../../i18n';
     6	import { ApiError, deleteSavedQuote, updateSavedQuoteNote } from '../../lib/api';
     7	import type { DocumentBiblioCsl, SavedQuote } from '../../lib/api';
     8	import { formatApaInText } from '../../lib/apaFormat';
     9	import { pageRangeLabel, tierLabel, trustLabel } from './utils';
    10	
    11	interface SavedQuoteCardProps {
    12	  quote: SavedQuote;
    13	  index: number;
    14	  biblio: DocumentBiblioCsl | null;
    15	  onJump: (quote: SavedQuote, index: number) => void;
    16	  onDeleted: (quoteId: string) => void;
    17	}
    18	
    19	/**
    20	 * A single saved quote in the Evidence Board (M3-F2). Renders the STORED
    21	 * snapshot fields (tier/score/source_kind taken at save time) — never
    22	 * re-verifies, matching the backend's own no-reverification-on-read
    23	 * guarantee (wave-m3-backend-report.md's B3 section): a saved quote's
    24	 * trust labels can go visually stale relative to a since-reparsed
    25	 * document, which is intentional v1 scope, not a bug.
    26	 */
    27	export default function SavedQuoteCard({ quote, index, biblio, onJump, onDeleted }: SavedQuoteCardProps) {
    28	  const { tOr } = useLocale();
    29	  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
    30	  const [note, setNote] = useState(quote.note || '');
    31	  const [savingNote, setSavingNote] = useState(false);
    32	  const [noteError, setNoteError] = useState(false);
    33	  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false);
    34	
    35	  // The quote object is the parent's source of truth; re-sync the local
    36	  // draft if it changes out from under us (e.g. a PATCH from elsewhere).
    37	  useEffect(() => {
    38	    setNote(quote.note || '');
    39	  }, [quote.id, quote.note]);
    40	
    41	  const handleCopy = async () => {
    42	    const apaInText = formatApaInText(biblio, quote.page);
    43	    const text = `"${quote.quoteText}" ${apaInText}`;
    44	    try {
    45	      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    46	      setCopied(true);
    47	      window.setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    48	    } catch {
    49	      // Best-effort, no toast — matches QuoteResultCard's copy handling.
    50	    }
    51	  };
    52	
    53	  const handleNoteBlur = async () => {
    54	    const trimmed = note.trim();
    55	    const original = quote.note || '';
    56	    if (trimmed === original) return; // no-op PATCH avoidance
    57	    setSavingNote(true);
    58	    setNoteError(false);
    59	    try {
    60	      await updateSavedQuoteNote(quote.id, trimmed || null);
    61	    } catch {
    62	      // Revert to last-known-good so the textarea doesn't silently claim a
    63	      // save that didn't happen.
    64	      setNote(original);
    65	      setNoteError(true);
    66	    } finally {
    67	      setSavingNote(false);
    68	    }
    69	  };
    70	
    71	  const handleDelete = async () => {
    72	    if (deleting) return;
    73	    setDeleting(true);
    74	    try {
    75	      await deleteSavedQuote(quote.id);
    76	      onDeleted(quote.id);
    77	    } catch (err) {
    78	      // 404 SAVED_QUOTE_NOT_FOUND means it's already gone (e.g. deleted
    79	      // from another tab) — treat that as success rather than leaving a
    80	      // ghost row the user can't get rid of.
    81	      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404) {
    82	        onDeleted(quote.id);
    83	        return;
    84	      }
    85	      setDeleting(false);
    86	    }
    87	  };
    88	
    89	  return (
    90	    <div className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--reader-border)] bg-[var(--reader-panel-solid)] px-4 py-3">
    91	      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
    92	        <span className="rounded-full bg-zinc-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300">
    93	          {tierLabel(quote.tier, tOr)}
    94	        </span>
    95	        <span
    96	          className={
    97	            quote.sourceKind === 'page_text'
    98	              ? 'text-[11px] text-[var(--reader-muted)]'
    99	              : 'inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-amber-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-950/30 dark:text-amber-200'
   100	          }
   101	        >
   102	          {trustLabel(quote.sourceKind, tOr)}
   103	        </span>
   104	      </div>
   105	
   106	      <blockquote className="mt-2 border-l-2 border-blue-600 pl-3 text-sm italic leading-relaxed text-[var(--reader-ink)] dark:border-blue-400">
   107	        &ldquo;{quote.quoteText}&rdquo;
   108	      </blockquote>
   109	
   110	      <div className="mt-2">
   111	        <textarea
   112	          value={note}
   113	          onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}
   114	          onBlur={() => void handleNoteBlur()}
   115	          maxLength={2000}
   116	          rows={2}
   117	          placeholder={tOr('quoteFinder.notePlaceholder', 'Add a note...')}
   118	          aria-label={tOr('quoteFinder.notePlaceholder', 'Add a note...')}
   119	          className="w-full resize-y rounded-md border border-[var(--reader-border)] bg-[var(--reader-panel-solid)] px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-[var(--reader-ink)] outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-500"
   120	        />
   121	        {savingNote ? (
   122	          <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-[var(--reader-muted)]">{tOr('quoteFinder.noteSaving', 'Saving note...')}</p>
   123	        ) : noteError ? (
   124	          <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-red-700 dark:text-red-300">{tOr('quoteFinder.noteSaveError', 'Failed to save note')}</p>
   125	        ) : null}
   126	      </div>
   127	
   128	      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
   129	        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-[var(--reader-muted)]">
   130	          <MapPin size={12} aria-hidden="true" />
   131	          <span>{pageRangeLabel(quote, tOr)}</span>
   132	        </div>
   133	        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   134	          <button
   135	            type="button"
   136	            onClick={() => onJump(quote, index)}
   137	            className="inline-flex min-h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-[var(--reader-evidence-border)] bg-[var(--reader-evidence-soft)] px-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-[var(--reader-evidence)] transition-colors hover:brightness-95 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-500"
   138	          >
   139	            <MapPin size={12} aria-hidden="true" />
   140	            {tOr('quoteFinder.jump', 'Jump to page')}
   141	          </button>
   142	          <button
   143	            type="button"
   144	            onClick={() => void handleCopy()}
   145	            className="inline-flex min-h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-[var(--reader-border)] bg-[var(--reader-panel-solid)] px-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-[var(--reader-ink)] transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--reader-panel-muted)] focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-500"
   146	          >
   147	            {copied ? <Check size={12} className="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" aria-hidden="true" /> : <Copy size={12} aria-hidden="true" />}
   148	            {copied ? tOr('quoteFinder.copied', 'Copied') : tOr('quoteFinder.copy', 'Copy quote + citation')}
   149	          </button>
   150	          <button
   151	            type="button"
   152	            onClick={() => void handleDelete()}
   153	            disabled={deleting}
   154	            aria-label={tOr('quoteFinder.delete', 'Delete')}
   155	            title={tOr('quoteFinder.delete', 'Delete')}
   156	            className="inline-flex min-h-8 min-w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-[var(--reader-border)] bg-[var(--reader-panel-solid)] text-[var(--reader-muted)] transition-colors hover:border-red-200 hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-700 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-blue-500 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 dark:hover:border-red-900/50 dark:hover:bg-red-950/30 dark:hover:text-red-300"
   157	          >
   158	            {deleting ? <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none" aria-hidden="true" /> : <Trash2 size={14} aria-hidden="true" />}
   159	          </button>
   160	        </div>
   161	      </div>
   162	    </div>
   163	  );
   164	}

    11	
    12	const TABS: Array<{ key: string; labelKey: string; fallback: string; icon: LucideIcon }> = [
    13	  { key: "profile", labelKey: "profile.tabs.profile", fallback: "Profile", icon: User },
    14	  { key: "credits", labelKey: "profile.tabs.credits", fallback: "Credits", icon: CreditCard },
    15	  { key: "usage", labelKey: "profile.tabs.usage", fallback: "Usage", icon: BarChart3 },

    17	  { key: "account", labelKey: "profile.tabs.account", fallback: "Account", icon: Settings },
    18	  { key: "notifications", labelKey: "profile.tabs.notifications", fallback: "Notifications", icon: Bell },
    19	];
    20	
    21	export default function ProfileTabs({ activeTab, onChange }: Props) {
    22	  const { tOr } = useLocale();
    23	
    24	  return (
    25	    <>
    26	      {/* Mobile: horizontal tabs */}
    27	      <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-2 shadow-sm dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 md:hidden" role="tablist">
    28	        {TABS.map((tab) => {
    29	          const isActive = activeTab === tab.key;
    30	          return (
    31	            <button
    32	              key={tab.key}
    33	              type="button"
    34	              role="tab"
    35	              id={`profile-tab-${tab.key}`}
    36	              aria-controls={`profile-panel-${tab.key}`}
    37	              aria-selected={isActive}
    38	              onClick={() => onChange(tab.key)}
    39	              className={
    40	                `shrink-0 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-colors focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-zinc-400 dark:focus-visible:ring-zinc-500 focus-visible:ring-offset-2 dark:focus-visible:ring-offset-zinc-900 ` +
    41	                (isActive
    42	                  ? `bg-zinc-900 dark:bg-zinc-50 text-white dark:text-zinc-900`
    43	                  : `text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800`)
    44	              }
    45	            >
    46	              {tOr(tab.labelKey, tab.fallback)}
    47	            </button>

    52	      {/* Desktop (md+): vertical sidebar */}

    56	        aria-orientation="vertical"
    57	      >
    58	        {TABS.map((tab) => {

    67	              aria-controls={`profile-panel-${tab.key}`}
    68	              aria-selected={isActive}
    69	              onClick={() => onChange(tab.key)}
    70	              className={

    11	function SavedQuoteRow({
    12	  quote,
    13	  documentName,
    14	  onDeleted,
    15	}: {
    16	  quote: SavedQuote;
    17	  documentName: string;
    18	  onDeleted: (id: string) => void;
    19	}) {
    20	  const { tOr } = useLocale();
    21	  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
    22	  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false);

    24	  const handleCopy = async () => {
    25	    // Lightweight-integration choice (see SavedQuotesSection docstring): a
    26	    // plain quote-text copy, no APA citation — building a per-document
    27	    // biblio cache for a list that can span every document the user owns
    28	    // is exactly the complexity this board is meant to avoid in v1.
    29	    try {
    30	      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(quote.quoteText);
    31	      setCopied(true);
    32	      window.setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    33	    } catch {
    34	      // best-effort, no toast
    35	    }
    36	  };
    37	
    38	  const handleDelete = async () => {
    39	    if (deleting) return;
    40	    setDeleting(true);
    41	    try {
    42	      await deleteSavedQuote(quote.id);
    43	      onDeleted(quote.id);
    44	    } catch (err) {
    45	      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404) {
    46	        onDeleted(quote.id);
    47	        return;
    48	      }
    49	      setDeleting(false);
    50	    }
    51	  };

    54	    <div className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900">

    61	          {documentName}
    62	        </Link>
    63	        <span className="text-zinc-300 dark:text-zinc-700" aria-hidden="true">·</span>
    64	        <span className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">{pageRangeLabel(quote, tOr)}</span>
    65	        <span className="rounded-full bg-zinc-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300">

    67	        </span>

    73	          }
    74	        >
    75	          {trustLabel(quote.sourceKind, tOr)}
    76	        </span>
    77	      </div>
    78	
    79	      <blockquote className="mt-2 border-l-2 border-blue-600 pl-3 text-sm italic leading-relaxed text-zinc-900 dark:border-blue-400 dark:text-zinc-100">
    80	        &ldquo;{quote.quoteText}&rdquo;
    81	      </blockquote>
    82	
    83	      {quote.note ? (
    84	        <p className="mt-2 rounded-md bg-zinc-50 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs leading-5 text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-400">
    85	          {quote.note}
    86	        </p>
    87	      ) : null}
    88	
    89	      <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
    90	        <button
    91	          type="button"
    92	          onClick={() => void handleCopy()}
    93	          className="inline-flex min-h-8 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-zinc-200 bg-white px-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 transition-colors hover:bg-zinc-50 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-zinc-400 dark:border-zinc-700 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
    94	        >
    95	          {copied ? <Check size={12} className="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" aria-hidden="true" /> : <Copy size={12} aria-hidden="true" />}
    96	          {copied ? tOr("quoteFinder.copied", "Copied") : tOr("quoteFinder.copyText", "Copy quote")}
    97	        </button>
    98	        <button
    99	          type="button"

   112	
   113	/**
   114	 * The all-documents Evidence Board (M3-F2's "lightweight" half). Chose a
   115	 * new tab in the existing Profile page over a standalone route: the
   116	 * profile already has an established tabbed-sections pattern (Profile /
   117	 * Credits / Usage / Account / Notifications, see ProfileTabs.tsx) with its
   118	 * own auth guard and layout, and "every saved quote across all my
   119	 * documents" reads naturally as an account-level view alongside Credits/
   120	 * Usage rather than a document-scoped one — a new top-level route would
   121	 * duplicate that auth+layout scaffolding for no real benefit.
   122	 *
   123	 * Deliberately lighter than the per-document Saved tab inside Quote
   124	 * Finder (SavedQuoteList/SavedQuoteCard): read-only note display (no
   125	 * inline edit — that's a document-in-context action), plain "Copy quote"
   126	 * with no APA citation (avoids needing a biblio fetch per distinct
   127	 * document in the list), and "Open document" just links to the right
   128	 * page rather than restoring the fine-grained bbox highlight (that needs
   129	 * a live store, which only exists on the document reader route). Delete
   130	 * is still included since it's useful from anywhere and cheap either way.
   131	 */
   132	const FREE_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT = 30;
   133	
   134	export default function SavedQuotesSection({ userPlan }: { userPlan?: string }) {
   135	  const { t, tOr } = useLocale();
   136	  const [quotes, setQuotes] = useState<SavedQuote[] | null>(null);
   137	  const [documentNames, setDocumentNames] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
   138	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   139	  const [error, setError] = useState(false);
   140	
   141	  useEffect(() => {
   142	    let cancelled = false;
   143	    setLoading(true);
   144	    setError(false);
   145	    Promise.all([listAllSavedQuotes(), getMyDocuments()])
   146	      .then(([savedQuotes, documents]) => {
   147	        if (cancelled) return;
   148	        setQuotes(savedQuotes);
   149	        setDocumentNames(Object.fromEntries(documents.map((d) => [d.id, d.filename])));
   150	      })
   151	      .catch(() => {
   152	        if (!cancelled) setError(true);
   153	      })
   154	      .finally(() => {
   155	        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
   156	      });
   157	    return () => {
   158	      cancelled = true;
   159	    };
   160	  }, []);
   161	
   162	  const handleDeleted = (id: string) => {
   163	    setQuotes((prev) => (prev ? prev.filter((q) => q.id !== id) : prev));
   164	  };
   165	
   166	  if (loading) {
   167	    return <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">{t("common.loading")}</p>;
   168	  }
   169	
   170	  if (error) {
   171	    return (
   172	      <div className="rounded-lg bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700 dark:bg-red-900/20 dark:text-red-300">
   173	        {t("error.somethingWrong")}
   174	      </div>
   175	    );
   176	  }
   177	
   178	  if (!quotes || quotes.length === 0) {
   179	    return (
   180	      <div className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-10 text-center dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900">
   181	        <Bookmark aria-hidden size={28} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500" />
   182	        <h2 className="mb-1 text-base font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
   183	          {tOr("profile.savedQuotes.emptyTitle", "No saved quotes yet")}
   184	        </h2>
   185	        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
   186	          {tOr("profile.savedQuotes.emptyBody", "Save verified quotes from any document's Quote Finder panel to build your research library here.")}
   187	        </p>
   188	      </div>
   189	    );
   190	  }
   191	
   192	  return (
   193	    <div>
   194	      <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
   195	        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
   196	          {tOr("profile.savedQuotes.count", "{count} saved quotes", { count: quotes.length })}
   197	        </p>
   198	        {(userPlan || "free") === "free" ? (
   199	          // M3-F3: this board's own fetch already returns the TRUE global
   200	          // count (it's the same GET /api/quotes the cap itself is
   201	          // enforced against), so unlike the per-document panel tab, no
   202	          // extra request is needed here to show it honestly.
   203	          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
   204	            {tOr("quoteFinder.capIndicator", "{count} of {limit} saved", { count: quotes.length, limit: FREE_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT })}
   205	          </p>
   206	        ) : null}
   207	      </div>
   208	      <div className="space-y-3">
   209	        {quotes.map((quote) => (
   210	          <SavedQuoteRow
   211	            key={quote.id}
   212	            quote={quote}
   213	            documentName={documentNames[quote.documentId] || tOr("profile.savedQuotes.unknownDocument", "Untitled document")}
   214	            onDeleted={handleDeleted}
   215	          />
   216	        ))}
   217	      </div>
   218	    </div>
   219	  );
   220	}

   321	// --- Document biblio API (M2, plan §8.4.4 — minimal per-user citation metadata) ---
   322	
   323	export interface CslAuthor {
   324	  family?: string;
   325	  given?: string;
   326	}
   327	
   328	export interface DocumentBiblioCsl {
   329	  title?: string;
   330	  author?: CslAuthor[];
   331	  issued?: { year?: number };
   332	  [key: string]: unknown;
   333	}
   334	
   335	export interface DocumentBiblio {
   336	  cslJson: DocumentBiblioCsl;
   337	  source: 'system' | 'user' | string;
   338	}
   339	
   340	export async function getDocumentBiblio(documentId: string): Promise<DocumentBiblio> {
   341	  const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE}/api/documents/${documentId}/biblio`);
   342	  const data: any = await handle(res);
   343	  return { cslJson: data.csl_json || {}, source: data.source };
   344	}
   345	
   346	export async function updateDocumentBiblio(documentId: string, cslJson: DocumentBiblioCsl): Promise<DocumentBiblio> {
   347	  const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE}/api/documents/${documentId}/biblio`, {
   348	    method: 'PUT',
   349	    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   350	    body: JSON.stringify({ csl_json: cslJson }),
   351	  });
   352	  const data: any = await handle(res);
   353	  return { cslJson: data.csl_json || {}, source: data.source };
   354	}

   367	  verifierVersion: string;
   368	  sourceKind: 'page_text' | 'extracted_text' | string;
   369	  note: string | null;
   370	  createdAt: string;
   371	  updatedAt: string;
   372	}
   373	
   374	function mapSavedQuote(data: any): SavedQuote {
   375	  return {
   376	    id: data.id,
   377	    documentId: data.document_id,
   378	    page: data.page,
   379	    pageEnd: data.page_end,
   380	    quoteText: data.quote_text,
   381	    bboxes: data.bboxes || [],
   382	    tier: data.tier,
   383	    score: data.score,
   384	    verifierVersion: data.verifier_version,
   385	    sourceKind: data.source_kind,
   386	    note: data.note ?? null,
   387	    createdAt: data.created_at,
   388	    updatedAt: data.updated_at,
   389	  };
   390	}
   391	
   392	/**
   393	 * Saves a verified quote card. The server does NOT trust chunkId/quoteText
   394	 * for tier/score/page/bboxes — it re-derives every trust field itself
   395	 * (`quote_search_service.verify_saved_quote`), so only send back what a
   396	 * `QuoteCard` already gave you: `chunkId`, `displayText` -> quoteText, and
   397	 * optionally `page` as a disambiguation hint (never trusted to fabricate a
   398	 * page on its own). Idempotent — re-saving an already-saved quote returns
   399	 * 201 with the EXISTING row, never a duplicate and never a 409, so the
   400	 * caller doesn't need to track save-state itself before calling this.
   401	 */
   402	export async function saveQuote(documentId: string, params: { chunkId: string; quoteText: string; pageHint?: number }): Promise<SavedQuote> {
   403	  const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE}/api/documents/${documentId}/quotes`, {
   404	    method: 'POST',
   405	    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   406	    body: JSON.stringify({
   407	      chunk_id: params.chunkId,
   408	      quote_text: params.quoteText,
   409	      page_hint: params.pageHint ?? null,
   410	    }),
   411	  });
   412	  const data: any = await handle(res);
   413	  return mapSavedQuote(data);
   414	}
   415	
   416	/** One document's saved quotes, newest first. */
   417	export async function listDocumentSavedQuotes(documentId: string): Promise<SavedQuote[]> {
   418	  const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE}/api/documents/${documentId}/quotes`);
   419	  const data: any = await handle(res);
   420	  return (Array.isArray(data.quotes) ? data.quotes : []).map(mapSavedQuote);
   421	}
   422	
   423	/** The Evidence Board feed — every saved quote for the authed user across
   424	 * ALL their documents, newest first. Rows carry `documentId` but not a
   425	 * filename/title — join against `getMyDocuments()`, per the backend's own
   426	 * documented contract, rather than adding a second round trip here. */
   427	export async function listAllSavedQuotes(): Promise<SavedQuote[]> {
   428	  const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE}/api/quotes`);
   429	  const data: any = await handle(res);
   430	  return (Array.isArray(data.quotes) ? data.quotes : []).map(mapSavedQuote);
   431	}
   432	
   433	/** Only `note` is editable — every other field is a verification snapshot
   434	 * server-side and this endpoint doesn't accept them at all. `note: null`
   435	 * clears it. */
   436	export async function updateSavedQuoteNote(savedQuoteId: string, note: string | null): Promise<SavedQuote> {
   437	  const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE}/api/quotes/${savedQuoteId}`, {
   438	    method: 'PATCH',
   439	    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   440	    body: JSON.stringify({ note }),
   441	  });
   442	  const data: any = await handle(res);
   443	  return mapSavedQuote(data);
   444	}
   445	
   446	/** Hard delete — frees a cap slot immediately, no undo. */
   447	export async function deleteSavedQuote(savedQuoteId: string): Promise<void> {
   448	  const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE}/api/quotes/${savedQuoteId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
   449	  if (!res.ok) await throwApiError(res);
   450	}
   451	
   452	export async function listSessions(docId: string): Promise<SessionListResponse> {
   453	  const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE}/api/documents/${docId}/sessions`);
   454	  return handle(res);
   455	}
   456	
   457	export async function deleteSession(sessionId: string): Promise<void> {
   458	  const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE}/api/sessions/${sessionId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
   459	  if (!res.ok) {
   460	    const text = await res.text();
   461	    throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}: ${text}`);
   462	  }
   463	}
   464	
   465	export async function deleteDocument(docId: string): Promise<void> {
   466	  const res = await fetch(`${PROXY_BASE}/api/documents/${docId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
   467	  if (!res.ok) {
   468	    const text = await res.text();
   469	    throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}: ${text}`);
   470	  }

   113	/**
   114	 * The all-documents Evidence Board (M3-F2's "lightweight" half). Chose a

   129	 * a live store, which only exists on the document reader route). Delete
   130	 * is still included since it's useful from anywhere and cheap either way.
   131	 */
   132	const FREE_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT = 30;
   133	
   134	export default function SavedQuotesSection({ userPlan }: { userPlan?: string }) {
   135	  const { t, tOr } = useLocale();
   136	  const [quotes, setQuotes] = useState<SavedQuote[] | null>(null);
   137	  const [documentNames, setDocumentNames] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
   138	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   139	  const [error, setError] = useState(false);
   140	
   141	  useEffect(() => {
   142	    let cancelled = false;
   143	    setLoading(true);
   144	    setError(false);
   145	    Promise.all([listAllSavedQuotes(), getMyDocuments()])
   146	      .then(([savedQuotes, documents]) => {
   147	        if (cancelled) return;
   148	        setQuotes(savedQuotes);
   149	        setDocumentNames(Object.fromEntries(documents.map((d) => [d.id, d.filename])));
   150	      })
   151	      .catch(() => {
   152	        if (!cancelled) setError(true);
   153	      })
   154	      .finally(() => {
   155	        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
   156	      });
   157	    return () => {
   158	      cancelled = true;
   159	    };
   160	  }, []);
   161	
   162	  const handleDeleted = (id: string) => {
   163	    setQuotes((prev) => (prev ? prev.filter((q) => q.id !== id) : prev));
   164	  };
   165	
   166	  if (loading) {
   167	    return <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">{t("common.loading")}</p>;
   168	  }
   169	
   170	  if (error) {
   171	    return (
   172	      <div className="rounded-lg bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700 dark:bg-red-900/20 dark:text-red-300">
   173	        {t("error.somethingWrong")}
   174	      </div>
   175	    );
   176	  }
   177	
   178	  if (!quotes || quotes.length === 0) {
   179	    return (
   180	      <div className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-10 text-center dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900">
   181	        <Bookmark aria-hidden size={28} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500" />
   182	        <h2 className="mb-1 text-base font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
   183	          {tOr("profile.savedQuotes.emptyTitle", "No saved quotes yet")}
   184	        </h2>
   185	        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
   186	          {tOr("profile.savedQuotes.emptyBody", "Save verified quotes from any document's Quote Finder panel to build your research library here.")}
   187	        </p>
   188	      </div>
   189	    );
   190	  }
   191	
   192	  return (
   193	    <div>
   194	      <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
   195	        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
   196	          {tOr("profile.savedQuotes.count", "{count} saved quotes", { count: quotes.length })}
   197	        </p>
   198	        {(userPlan || "free") === "free" ? (
   199	          // M3-F3: this board's own fetch already returns the TRUE global
   200	          // count (it's the same GET /api/quotes the cap itself is
   201	          // enforced against), so unlike the per-document panel tab, no
   202	          // extra request is needed here to show it honestly.
   203	          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
   204	            {tOr("quoteFinder.capIndicator", "{count} of {limit} saved", { count: quotes.length, limit: FREE_SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT })}
   205	          </p>
   206	        ) : null}
   207	      </div>
   208	      <div className="space-y-3">
   209	        {quotes.map((quote) => (
   210	          <SavedQuoteRow
   211	            key={quote.id}
   212	            quote={quote}
   213	            documentName={documentNames[quote.documentId] || tOr("profile.savedQuotes.unknownDocument", "Untitled document")}
   214	            onDeleted={handleDeleted}
   215	          />
   216	        ))}
   217	      </div>
   218	    </div>
   219	  );
   220	}

     1	"""Real-Postgres integration tests for saved_quotes (M3-B1/B2/B3, plan D8
     2	amended by §8.5 M3 / §8.1 / §8.4 point 2).
     3	
     4	Mocked-db unit tests (test_saved_quotes_service.py, test_saved_quotes_api.py)
     5	already cover the LOGIC; these prove the same behavior against a real
     6	database — a genuine UNIQUE (user_id, document_id, quote_hash) index under
     7	real concurrent inserts, and the ON DELETE SET NULL FK actually surviving a
     8	real chunk deletion (§8.1's "saved quotes must survive reparses"
     9	requirement, reproduced here as the parse worker's own deletion shape:
    10	`sa_delete(Chunk).where(Chunk.document_id == doc.id)`).
    11	
    12	Requires docker (Postgres) — SKIP_INTEGRATION=1 (the default) skips this
    13	whole file.
    14	"""

    16	
    17	import asyncio
    18	import sys

    31

    34	    from app.models.database import AsyncSessionLocal
    35	    from app.models.tables import Document
    36	
    37	    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
    38	        doc = Document(
    39	            filename="integration-test.pdf",
    40	            file_size=100,
    41	            storage_key=f"documents/{uuid.uuid4()}/integration-test.pdf",
    42	            status="ready",
    43	            user_id=user_id,
    44	        )
    45	        db.add(doc)
    46	        await db.commit()
    47	        await db.refresh(doc)
    48	        return doc.id
    49

    51	async def _create_chunk(document_id: uuid.UUID) -> uuid.UUID:

    55	    async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
    56	        chunk = Chunk(

    59	            token_count=12, page_start=4, page_end=4, bboxes=[],
    60	        )
    61	        db.add(chunk)

    67	async def _saved_quote_rows(user_id: uuid.UUID):

    86	class TestConcurrentIdenticalSaveNeverDuplicates:
    87	    async def test_two_concurrent_identical_saves_produce_exactly_one_row(
    88	        self, auth_user,
    89	    ) -> None:
    90	        """Codex-style deterministic-race reproduction: two requests for the
    91	        SAME quote (double-click, two tabs) both racing to INSERT. Mocked
    92	        tests can prove the retry LOGIC runs; only a real unique index can
    93	        prove the DATABASE actually stops the duplicate.
    94	
    95	        `return_exceptions=True` + a final settle step is deliberate, not a
    96	        weakened assertion: this project's test suite runs against NullPool
    97	        (TESTING=1, see app/models/database.py) so every checkout opens a
    98	        brand-new physical connection, and a truly concurrent
    99	        rollback-then-new-connection sequence under asyncio.gather can
   100	        surface a transient SQLAlchemy/asyncpg async-bridge hiccup
   101	        (`MissingGreenlet`) that is specific to NullPool under test —
   102	        reproduced standalone outside pytest, and confirmed ABSENT against
   103	        a production-shaped QueuePool-backed engine (the same pool
   104	        app/models/database.py uses whenever TESTING is unset). Treating
   105	        that as an acceptable outcome for a racing side, while still
   106	        requiring the DATABASE to end up in exactly one of the two
   107	        correct end-states every single round, is what actually matters:
   108	        the unique index is the thing under test, not this harness's pool
   109	        implementation."""
   110	        from app.models.database import AsyncSessionLocal
   111	        from app.services import saved_quotes_service
   112	
   113	        document_id = await _create_ready_document(auth_user.id)
   114	        chunk_id = await _create_chunk(document_id)
   115	        card = _card(chunk_id)
   116	        expected_hash = saved_quotes_service.compute_quote_hash(card.display_text, card.page, card.page_end)
   117	
   118	        async def _one_save():
   119	            from app.models.tables import Document
   120	
   121	            async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
   122	                document = await db.get(Document, document_id)
   123	                return await saved_quotes_service.save_quote(
   124	                    db, user=auth_user, document=document, card=card,
   125	                )
   126	
   127	        for _round in range(5):
   128	            results = await asyncio.gather(_one_save(), _one_save(), return_exceptions=True)
   129	            clean = [r for r in results if not isinstance(r, BaseException)]
   130	            # At least one side must land cleanly — a genuine failure on
   131	            # BOTH sides simultaneously would mean the row never got saved
   132	            # at all, which the settle step below cannot paper over.
   133	            assert clean, f"round {_round}: both concurrent saves raised: {results!r}"
   134	
   135	            # Whichever side (if either) hit the NullPool artifact above,
   136	            # settle deterministically: a plain, NON-concurrent save must
   137	            # always resolve to the SAME row via the idempotent path.
   138	            settled_row, _settled_created = await _one_save()
   139	
   140	            rows = await _saved_quote_rows(auth_user.id)
   141	            assert len(rows) == 1, f"round {_round}: expected exactly one row, got {len(rows)}"
   142	            assert rows[0].quote_hash == expected_hash
   143	            assert rows[0].id == settled_row.id
   144	            for row, _created in clean:
   145	                assert row.id == settled_row.id  # every clean result agreed on the SAME row
   146	
   147	            # Clean up between rounds so each round starts from "no row yet."
   148	            async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
   149	                from app.models.tables import SavedQuote
   150	
   151	                await db.execute(sa_delete(SavedQuote).where(SavedQuote.id == settled_row.id))
   152	                await db.commit()
   153	        assert rows[0].quote_hash == saved_quotes_service.compute_quote_hash(
   154	            card.display_text, card.page, card.page_end
   155	        )
   156	
   157	
   158	class TestSavedQuoteSurvivesChunkDeletion:
   159	    async def test_reparse_style_chunk_deletion_leaves_the_saved_row_intact(
   160	        self, auth_user,
   161	    ) -> None:
   162	        """§8.1: saved quotes must survive reparses. Reproduces the parse
   163	        worker's own deletion shape (parse_worker.py:184,
   164	        `sa_delete(Chunk).where(Chunk.document_id == doc.id)`) directly —
   165	        the FK's ON DELETE SET NULL must leave the saved_quotes row (and
   166	        every one of its snapshotted display fields) untouched, losing only
   167	        the now-dangling chunk reference."""
   168	        from app.models.database import AsyncSessionLocal
   169	        from app.models.tables import Chunk, Document, SavedQuote
   170	        from app.services import saved_quotes_service
   171	
   172	        document_id = await _create_ready_document(auth_user.id)
   173	        chunk_id = await _create_chunk(document_id)
   174	        card = _card(chunk_id)
   175	
   176	        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
   177	            document = await db.get(Document, document_id)
   178	            row, created = await saved_quotes_service.save_quote(
   179	                db, user=auth_user, document=document, card=card,
   180	            )
   181	            assert created is True
   182	            saved_quote_id = row.id
   183	
   184	        # Reproduce the parse worker's reparse-time chunk deletion exactly.
   185	        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
   186	            await db.execute(sa_delete(Chunk).where(Chunk.document_id == document_id))
   187	            await db.commit()
   188	
   189	        async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
   190	            survivor = await db.get(SavedQuote, saved_quote_id)

    27	def _card(**overrides) -> QuoteCard:
    28	    base = dict(
    29	        display_text="the exact quoted sentence",
    30	        page=4, page_end=4, bboxes=[{"x": 0.1, "y": 0.2, "w": 0.3, "h": 0.05, "page": 4}],
    31	        tier="exact", source_kind="extracted_text", chunk_id=str(uuid.uuid4()), score=100.0,
    32	        # M3 review addition (plan §8.1 anchor fields) — realistic values so
    33	        # save_quote()'s persistence of them is exercised by every test
    34	        # using this helper, not just a dedicated one.
    35	        source_text_hash="a" * 64, quote_start=11, quote_end=57,
    36	    )
    37	    base.update(overrides)
    38	    return QuoteCard(**base)

    41	class _ScalarResult:
    42	    def __init__(self, value=None, values=None):
    43	        self._value = value
    44	        self._values = values if values is not None else ([] if value is None else [value])
    45	
    46	    def scalar_one_or_none(self):
    47	        return self._value
    48	
    49	    def scalar(self):
    50	        return self._value
    51	
    52	    def scalars(self):
    53	        return SimpleNamespace(all=lambda: self._values)

    74	class TestComputeQuoteHash:
    75	    def test_deterministic_for_the_same_inputs(self) -> None:
    76	        h1 = sqs.compute_quote_hash("Hello world.", 3, 3)
    77	        h2 = sqs.compute_quote_hash("Hello world.", 3, 3)
    78	        assert h1 == h2
    79	
    80	    def test_normalizes_whitespace_and_quote_variants(self) -> None:
    81	        """§8.1's dedup philosophy: two honest saves of the "same" wording
    82	        that differ only in incidental formatting (smart quotes, extra
    83	        whitespace) must collide, or the unique constraint silently stops
    84	        protecting against duplicate saves."""
    85	        h1 = sqs.compute_quote_hash("“Hello   world.”", 3, 3)
    86	        h2 = sqs.compute_quote_hash('"Hello world."', 3, 3)
    87	        assert h1 == h2
    88	
    89	    def test_different_page_range_changes_the_hash(self) -> None:
    90	        """A client cannot dodge the unique constraint by claiming a fake
    91	        page for identical text, NOR can the same real text on two
    92	        genuinely different pages ever incorrectly collide."""
    93	        h1 = sqs.compute_quote_hash("Hello world.", 3, 3)
    94	        h2 = sqs.compute_quote_hash("Hello world.", 4, 4)
    95	        assert h1 != h2
    96	
    97	    def test_different_text_changes_the_hash(self) -> None:
    98	        h1 = sqs.compute_quote_hash("Hello world.", 3, 3)
    99	        h2 = sqs.compute_quote_hash("Goodbye world.", 3, 3)
   100	        assert h1 != h2

   103	class TestSaveQuoteIdempotency:
   104	    @pytest.mark.asyncio
   105	    async def test_first_save_inserts_a_new_row(self) -> None:
   106	        user = SimpleNamespace(id=uuid.uuid4())
   107	        document = SimpleNamespace(id=uuid.uuid4())
   108	        db = SimpleNamespace(
   109	            execute=AsyncMock(return_value=_ScalarResult(None)),
   110	            add=lambda _obj: None,
   111	            commit=AsyncMock(),
   112	            rollback=AsyncMock(),
   113	        )
   114	
   115	        row, created = await sqs.save_quote(db, user=user, document=document, card=_card())
   116	
   117	        assert created is True
   118	        assert row.user_id == user.id
   119	        assert row.document_id == document.id
   120	        assert row.verification_tier == "exact"
   121	        assert row.verification_score == 100.0
   122	        assert row.verifier_version == sqs.QUOTE_VERIFIER_VERSION
   123	        assert row.source_kind == "extracted_text"
   124	        # M3 review addition (plan §8.1 anchor fields): copied from the
   125	        # card as-is, same as every other trust field — save_quote() never
   126	        # recomputes them, only quote_search_service.verify_saved_quote()
   127	        # (or quote_search()'s own cards, which leave them None) does.

   134	    async def test_repeat_save_of_the_identical_quote_returns_existing_row_not_a_new_one(self) -> None:
   135	        """The idempotent-hit path must short-circuit BEFORE ever touching
   136	        db.add/commit — this is the "returns 200 not 409" contract from the
   137	        API layer's perspective."""
   138	        user = SimpleNamespace(id=uuid.uuid4())
   139	        document = SimpleNamespace(id=uuid.uuid4())

   143	            add=AsyncMock(side_effect=AssertionError("must not insert on an idempotent hit")),
   144	            commit=AsyncMock(),

   146	        )

   148	        row, created = await sqs.save_quote(db, user=user, document=document, card=_card())

   150	        assert created is False
   151	        assert row is existing_row
   152	        db.commit.assert_not_awaited()

   155	    async def test_concurrent_identical_save_recovers_via_integrity_error_retry(
   156	        self, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
   157	    ) -> None:
   158	        """Mirrors biblio_service's FIX-9 precedent: two concurrent identical
   159	        saves can both SELECT None then both attempt INSERT — the loser's
   160	        commit raises IntegrityError (the unique index), and it must
   161	        recover by re-fetching the winner's row, not raise or 500.
   162	
   163	        UNLIKE biblio_service's byte-for-byte pattern, the retry query runs
   164	        on a FRESH AsyncSessionLocal() session, not the just-rolled-back
   165	        `db` — discovered via real-Postgres asyncio.gather concurrency
   166	        testing (test_saved_quotes_integration.py) that reusing a
   167	        NullPool-backed session for a new query immediately after a
   168	        rollback races with SQLAlchemy's async/greenlet connection-checkout
   169	        machinery under true concurrent load (reproduced standalone outside
   170	        pytest too; a production QueuePool-backed engine did not exhibit
   171	        this). A fresh session sidesteps it unconditionally and matches the
   172	        independent-session-for-post-failure-resolution pattern already
   173	        used by the billing resolvers in chat_service.py / quotes.py."""
   174	        user = SimpleNamespace(id=uuid.uuid4())
   175	        document = SimpleNamespace(id=uuid.uuid4())
   176	        winner_row = SimpleNamespace(id=uuid.uuid4())
   177	        db = SimpleNamespace(
   178	            execute=AsyncMock(return_value=_ScalarResult(None)),
   179	            add=lambda _obj: None,
   180	            commit=AsyncMock(side_effect=IntegrityError("stmt", {}, Exception("unique violation"))),
   181	            rollback=AsyncMock(),
   182	        )
   183	
   184	        class _FakeRetrySession:
   185	            async def __aenter__(self_inner):
   186	                return SimpleNamespace(execute=AsyncMock(return_value=_ScalarResult(winner_row)))
   187	
   188	            async def __aexit__(self_inner, *exc):
   189	                return False
   190	
   191	        monkeypatch.setattr(sqs, "AsyncSessionLocal", lambda: _FakeRetrySession())
   192	
   193	        row, created = await sqs.save_quote(db, user=user, document=document, card=_card())
   194	
   195	        assert created is False
   196	        assert row is winner_row
   197	        db.rollback.assert_awaited_once()
   198

   264	        """Live E2E bug (2026-08-03): PATCH 500'd with MissingGreenlet
   265	        because `updated_at` (onupdate=sa.func.now()) gets marked EXPIRED
   266	        after the UPDATE flush and was read synchronously afterward
   267	        (quotes.py's _saved_quote_response) outside an awaited context.

   269	        before the UPDATE has landed would be pointless) and on the SAME
   270	        row instance, so every attribute is safely in-memory before any
   271	        caller touches it."""
   272	        row = SimpleNamespace(note=None)
   273	        calls: list[str] = []

   277	        )

codex
The existing real-Postgres concurrency test proves only identical-save deduplication; it does not exercise the cap under concurrent distinct inserts, despite the test module’s header saying it does. Separately, I’ve identified two candidate UI state leaks—card-local “Saved” state can survive into a different quote when React reuses the same chunk/index key, and failed Saved-tab refetches can reveal the prior document’s cached rows. I’m validating both against parent lifecycles before assigning severity.

   132	    method: 'POST',

   134	    body: form,

    93	    if sigs and not any(data[:len(sig)] == sig for sig in sigs):
    94	        return False

    99	                if '[Content_Types].xml' not in zf.namelist():

   102	                if total_uncompressed > _MAX_UNCOMPRESSED_SIZE:
   103	                    return False
   104	        except zipfile.BadZipFile:
   105	            return False
   106	    return True

     1	"""Quote Finder verification gate (D1) — "LLM proposes, verifier disposes,
     2	source displays."
     3	
     4	The LLM only *proposes* a quote string. This service locates that string in the
     5	stored source text (the cited chunk's text, ± retrieved neighbours) and returns
     6	the RAW source slice for display. The LLM emission is never shown. Three tiers:
     7	
     8	1. ``exact``      — exact substring of the raw source.
     9	2. ``normalized`` — exact substring after identical offset-preserving
    10	   normalization (folds quote/dash/ligature/width/whitespace variation),
    11	   projected back to raw offsets.
    12	3. ``aligned``    — rapidfuzz ``partial_ratio_alignment`` locates the best
    13	   window; auto-accepted only above ``auto_cutoff`` AND past the length /
    14	   length-ratio / document-quality guards. ``flagged`` when a fuzzy match is
    15	   credible but a guard withholds auto-accept; ``dropped`` below ``flag_cutoff``
    16	   or when the proposal cannot be located.
    17	
    18	Guards (plan §8.1): short quotes over-match under fuzzy alignment, and a
    19	garbled (OCR / low ``text_quality``) source cannot anchor a verbatim guarantee,
    20	so fuzzy auto-accept is withheld in those cases. Deterministic substring tiers
    21	(exact / normalized) are always trusted regardless of document quality.
    22	"""
    23	from __future__ import annotations
    24	
    25	from dataclasses import dataclass
    26	from typing import Optional
    27	
    28	from rapidfuzz import fuzz
    29	
    30	from app.services.text_normalizer import normalize, raw_span, span_on_raw_boundaries
    31	
    32	# Default thresholds — see plan §8.4(5).
    33	AUTO_CUTOFF = 95.0  # fuzzy score at/above which we may auto-accept
    34	FLAG_CUTOFF = 90.0  # below this a fuzzy match is dropped entirely
    35	MIN_CHARS = 40      # shorter quotes never auto-accept on fuzzy alone
    36	MIN_TOKENS = 8
    37	LEN_RATIO_LO = 0.6  # matched-slice length vs proposed length sanity band
    38	LEN_RATIO_HI = 1.67
    39	MIN_COVERAGE = 0.95  # fraction of the PROPOSED quote that must align (anti-trim)
    40	LOW_QUALITY_THRESHOLD = 0.75
    41	
    42	# M3-B3 (plan §8.1/§8.5): saved_quotes snapshots this alongside a card's
    43	# tier/score at save time. Bump whenever this module's tiering/threshold/
    44	# guard logic changes materially — a future "revalidate saved quotes"
    45	# fast-follow can then tell an old snapshot apart from one produced by the
    46	# current algorithm. Purely informational in v1: nothing re-verifies a
    47	# saved quote against this value yet.
    48	QUOTE_VERIFIER_VERSION = "v1"
    49	
    50	_VERIFIED_STATUSES = ("exact", "normalized", "aligned")
    51	
    52	
    53	@dataclass
    54	class QuoteVerification:
    55	    status: str  # exact | normalized | aligned | flagged | dropped
    56	    display_text: Optional[str]  # raw source slice, or None when dropped
    57	    raw_start: Optional[int]
    58	    raw_end: Optional[int]
    59	    score: float
    60	    reason: Optional[str] = None
    61	
    62	    @property
    63	    def verified(self) -> bool:
    64	        """Hard caller contract: render as a quote card ONLY when True.
    65	        ``flagged`` carries display text for an optional confirmation UI but is
    66	        never a verified quote; ``dropped`` carries nothing."""
    67	        return self.status in _VERIFIED_STATUSES
    68	
    69	
    70	_DROPPED = QuoteVerification(
    71	    status="dropped", display_text=None, raw_start=None, raw_end=None, score=0.0
    72	)
    73	
    74	
    75	def _quality_caps_fuzzy(text_quality: Optional[float], parse_method: Optional[str]) -> bool:
    76	    """A garbled source cannot back a verbatim guarantee via fuzzy matching."""
    77	    if text_quality is not None and text_quality < LOW_QUALITY_THRESHOLD:
    78	        return True
    79	    if parse_method == "ocr" and text_quality is None:
    80	        return True
    81	    return False
    82	
    83	
    84	def verify_quote(
    85	    proposed: str,
    86	    source_text: str,
    87	    *,
    88	    text_quality: Optional[float] = None,
    89	    parse_method: Optional[str] = None,
    90	    auto_cutoff: float = AUTO_CUTOFF,
    91	    flag_cutoff: float = FLAG_CUTOFF,
    92	    min_chars: int = MIN_CHARS,
    93	    min_tokens: int = MIN_TOKENS,
    94	) -> QuoteVerification:
    95	    proposed = (proposed or "").strip()
    96	    if not proposed or not source_text:
    97	        return QuoteVerification("dropped", None, None, None, 0.0, "empty")
    98	
    99	    # Tier 1: exact substring of the raw source.
   100	    idx = source_text.find(proposed)
   101	    if idx != -1:
   102	        return QuoteVerification(
   103	            "exact", source_text[idx : idx + len(proposed)], idx, idx + len(proposed), 100.0
   104	        )
   105	
   106	    # Tier 2: exact substring in normalized space, projected back to raw.
   107	    # The match must span WHOLE raw code points — a match ending inside one
   108	    # code point's expansion (… → ...) is not a real source substring.
   109	    src_norm, src_map = normalize(source_text)
   110	    pq_norm, _ = normalize(proposed)
   111	    if pq_norm:
   112	        nidx = src_norm.find(pq_norm)
   113	        while nidx != -1:
   114	            nend = nidx + len(pq_norm)
   115	            if span_on_raw_boundaries(src_map, nidx, nend):
   116	                rs, re = raw_span(src_map, len(source_text), nidx, nend)
   117	                return QuoteVerification(
   118	                    "normalized", source_text[rs:re].strip(), rs, re, 100.0
   119	                )
   120	            nidx = src_norm.find(pq_norm, nidx + 1)
   121	
   122	    # Tier 3: rapidfuzz alignment over the fuzzy normalization.
   123	    src_fnorm, src_fmap = normalize(source_text, fuzzy=True)
   124	    pq_fnorm, _ = normalize(proposed, fuzzy=True)
   125	    if not pq_fnorm or not src_fnorm:
   126	        return _DROPPED
   127	
   128	    align = fuzz.partial_ratio_alignment(pq_fnorm, src_fnorm, score_cutoff=flag_cutoff)
   129	    if align is None:
   130	        return _DROPPED
   131	
   132	    rs, re = raw_span(src_fmap, len(source_text), align.dest_start, align.dest_end)
   133	    display = source_text[rs:re].strip()
   134	    if not display:
   135	        return _DROPPED
   136	
   137	    too_short = len(proposed) < min_chars or len(proposed.split()) < min_tokens
   138	    ratio = len(display) / len(proposed)
   139	    ratio_ok = LEN_RATIO_LO <= ratio <= LEN_RATIO_HI
   140	    capped = _quality_caps_fuzzy(text_quality, parse_method)
   141	    # Coverage = fraction of the PROPOSED quote that aligned. partial_ratio can
   142	    # score 100 over only a substring of the proposal, silently trimming
   143	    # invented prefix/suffix text. A verbatim guarantee requires (nearly) the
   144	    # whole proposal to be found.
   145	    coverage = (align.src_end - align.src_start) / len(pq_fnorm) if pq_fnorm else 0.0
   146	    covered = coverage >= MIN_COVERAGE
   147	
   148	    would_auto = align.score >= auto_cutoff and ratio_ok and covered
   149	    if would_auto and not too_short and not capped:
   150	        return QuoteVerification("aligned", display, rs, re, float(align.score))
   151	
   152	    reason = (
   153	        "low_quality" if capped else "too_short" if too_short
   154	        else "coverage" if not covered
   155	        else "len_ratio" if not ratio_ok else "below_auto"
   156	    )
   157	    return QuoteVerification("flagged", display, rs, re, float(align.score), reason)

    49	async def _resolve_active_subscription_id(user: User) -> Optional[str]:
    50	    if user.stripe_subscription_id and user.stripe_subscription_id != "pending":
    51	        return user.stripe_subscription_id
    52	    if not user.stripe_customer_id:
    53	        return None
    54	
    55	    subs = await asyncio.to_thread(
    56	        stripe.Subscription.list,
    57	        customer=user.stripe_customer_id,
    58	        status="all",
    59	        limit=10,
    60	    )
    61	    for sub in getattr(subs, "data", []) or []:
    62	        if sub.get("status") in _CANCELLABLE_SUBSCRIPTION_STATUSES:
    63	            return sub.get("id")
    64	    return None

    78	@router.get("/profile", response_model=UserProfileResponse)
    79	async def get_profile(
    80	    user: User = Depends(require_auth),
    81	    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db_session),
    82	):
    83	    cache_key = f"user:profile:{user.id}"
    84	    cached_profile = await cache_get(cache_key)
    85	
    86	    connected_accounts: list[dict]
    87	    stats: dict[str, int]
    88	    if isinstance(cached_profile, dict) and isinstance(cached_profile.get("connected_accounts"), list) and isinstance(cached_profile.get("stats"), dict):
    89	        connected_accounts = cached_profile["connected_accounts"]
    90	        stats = cached_profile["stats"]
    91	    else:
    92	        # Connected accounts
    93	        acc_rows = await db.execute(select(Account).where(Account.user_id == user.id))
    94	        accounts: List[Account] = acc_rows.scalars().all()
    95	
    96	        # Aggregated stats (single round-trip for core counts/sums)
    97	        stats_row = (
    98	            await db.execute(
    99	                select(
   100	                    func.coalesce(
   101	                        select(func.count())
   102	                        .select_from(Document)
   103	                        .where(Document.user_id == user.id)
   104	                        .scalar_subquery(),
   105	                        0,
   106	                    ).label("total_documents"),
   107	                    func.coalesce(
   108	                        select(func.count())
   109	                        .select_from(ChatSession)
   110	                        .join(Document, ChatSession.document_id == Document.id)
   111	                        .where(Document.user_id == user.id)
   112	                        .scalar_subquery(),
   113	                        0,
   114	                    ).label("total_sessions"),
   115	                    func.coalesce(
   116	                        select(func.count())
   117	                        .select_from(Message)
   118	                        .join(ChatSession, Message.session_id == ChatSession.id)
   119	                        .join(Document, ChatSession.document_id == Document.id)
   120	                        .where(Document.user_id == user.id)
   121	                        .scalar_subquery(),
   122	                        0,
   123	                    ).label("total_messages"),
   124	                    func.coalesce(
   125	                        select(func.sum(-CreditLedger.delta))
   126	                        .where(CreditLedger.user_id == user.id)
   127	                        .where(CreditLedger.delta < 0)
   128	                        .scalar_subquery(),
   129	                        0,
   130	                    ).label("total_credits_spent"),
   131	                    func.coalesce(
   132	                        select(func.sum(UsageRecord.total_tokens))
   133	                        .where(UsageRecord.user_id == user.id)
   134	                        .scalar_subquery(),
   135	                        0,
   136	                    ).label("total_tokens_used"),
   137	                )
   138	            )
   139	        ).one()
   140	
   141	        connected_accounts = [
   142	            {
   143	                "provider": acc.provider,
   144	                # Account model has no created_at column; return None for compatibility
   145	                "created_at": None,
   146	            }
   147	            for acc in accounts
   148	        ]
   149	        stats = {
   150	            "total_documents": int(stats_row.total_documents or 0),
   151	            "total_sessions": int(stats_row.total_sessions or 0),
   152	            "total_messages": int(stats_row.total_messages or 0),
   153	            "total_credits_spent": int(stats_row.total_credits_spent or 0),
   154	            "total_tokens_used": int(stats_row.total_tokens_used or 0),
   155	        }
   156	        await cache_set(
   157	            cache_key,
   158	            {"connected_accounts": connected_accounts, "stats": stats},
   159	            ttl_seconds=30,
   160	        )
   161	
   162	    # Monthly allowance by plan
   163	    plan = (user.plan or "free").lower()
   164	    if plan == "pro":
   165	        monthly_allowance = int(settings.PLAN_PRO_MONTHLY_CREDITS or 0)
   166	    elif plan == "plus":
   167	        monthly_allowance = int(settings.PLAN_PLUS_MONTHLY_CREDITS or 0)
   168	    else:
   169	        monthly_allowance = int(settings.PLAN_FREE_MONTHLY_CREDITS or 0)
   170	
   171	    billing_state = await compute_billing_state(user)
   172	
   173	    return {
