   192	@router.get("/usage-breakdown", response_model=UsageBreakdownResponse)
   193	async def get_usage_breakdown(
   194	    user: User = Depends(require_auth),
   195	    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db_session),
   196	):
   197	    rows = await db.execute(
   198	        select(
   199	            UsageRecord.model.label("model"),
   200	            func.count(UsageRecord.id).label("total_calls"),
   201	            func.coalesce(func.sum(UsageRecord.cost_credits), 0).label("total_credits"),
   202	        )
   203	        .where(UsageRecord.user_id == user.id)
   204	        .group_by(UsageRecord.model)
   205	    )
   206	    results = rows.all()
   207	
   208	    # Aggregate by mode
   209	    mode_agg: dict[str, dict] = {}
   210	    for r in results:
   211	        mode = MODEL_TO_MODE.get(r.model, "other")
   212	        if mode not in mode_agg:
   213	            mode_agg[mode] = {"total_calls": 0, "total_credits": 0}
   214	        mode_agg[mode]["total_calls"] += int(r.total_calls or 0)
   215	        mode_agg[mode]["total_credits"] += int(r.total_credits or 0)
   216	
   217	    grand_total_credits = sum(m["total_credits"] for m in mode_agg.values())
   218	
   219	    # Sort order: current modes first, legacy Thorough next, then "other" last.
   220	    mode_order = {"quick": 0, "balanced": 1, "thorough": 2}
   221	    sorted_modes = sorted(mode_agg.keys(), key=lambda m: mode_order.get(m, 99))
   222	
   223	    return {
   224	        "by_mode": [
   225	            {
   226	                "mode": mode,
   227	                "total_calls": mode_agg[mode]["total_calls"],
   228	                "total_credits": mode_agg[mode]["total_credits"],
   229	                "avg_credits_per_chat": round(mode_agg[mode]["total_credits"] / mode_agg[mode]["total_calls"])
   230	                if mode_agg[mode]["total_calls"] > 0
   231	                else 0,
   232	                "share": round(mode_agg[mode]["total_credits"] / grand_total_credits * 100, 1)
   233	                if grand_total_credits > 0
   234	                else 0,
   235	            }
   236	            for mode in sorted_modes
   237	        ]
   238	    }
   239	
   240	
   241	_export_rate_limit: dict[str, float] = {}  # user_id -> last export timestamp
   242	EXPORT_COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 3600  # 1 hour
   243	
   244	
   245	@router.get("/me/export")
   246	async def export_my_data(
   247	    user: User = Depends(require_auth),
   248	    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db_session),
   249	):
   250	    """Export all user data as JSON (GDPR Art. 20 data portability)."""
   251	    uid = str(user.id)
   252	    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp()
   253	    last_export = _export_rate_limit.get(uid, 0)
   254	    if now - last_export < EXPORT_COOLDOWN_SECONDS:
   255	        remaining = int(EXPORT_COOLDOWN_SECONDS - (now - last_export))
   256	        return Response(
   257	            content=json.dumps({"error": "EXPORT_RATE_LIMITED", "retry_after": remaining}),
   258	            status_code=429,
   259	            media_type="application/json",
   260	            headers={"Retry-After": str(remaining)},
   261	        )
   262	
   263	    # 1. User profile
   264	    profile = {
   265	        "id": uid,
   266	        "email": user.email,
   267	        "name": user.name,
   268	        "plan": user.plan,
   269	        "created_at": user.created_at.isoformat() if user.created_at else None,
   270	        "credits_balance": user.credits_balance,
   271	    }
   272	
   273	    # 2. Documents metadata
   274	    docs_result = await db.execute(
   275	        select(Document)
   276	        .where(Document.user_id == user.id)
   277	        .options(selectinload(Document.sessions).selectinload(ChatSession.messages))
   278	        .order_by(Document.created_at)
   279	    )
   280	    docs = docs_result.scalars().unique().all()
   281	    documents = [
   282	        {
   283	            "id": str(d.id),
   284	            "filename": d.filename,
   285	            "file_type": getattr(d, "file_type", "pdf"),
   286	            "status": d.status,
   287	            "created_at": d.created_at.isoformat() if d.created_at else None,
   288	            "page_count": d.page_count,
   289	        }
   290	        for d in docs
   291	    ]
   292	
   293	    # 3. Sessions and messages
   294	    conversations = []
   295	    for d in docs:
   296	        for sess in d.sessions:
   297	            sorted_messages = sorted(
   298	                sess.messages,
   299	                key=lambda m: m.created_at or datetime.min.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc),
   300	            )
   301	            conversations.append({
   302	                "session_id": str(sess.id),
   303	                "document_id": str(d.id),
   304	                "title": sess.title,
   305	                "created_at": sess.created_at.isoformat() if sess.created_at else None,
   306	                "messages": [
   307	                    {
   308	                        "role": m.role,
   309	                        "content": m.content,
   310	                        "created_at": m.created_at.isoformat() if m.created_at else None,
   311	                    }
   312	                    for m in sorted_messages
   313	                ],
   314	            })
   315	
   316	    # 4. Credit history
   317	    ledger_rows = await db.execute(
   318	        select(CreditLedger)
   319	        .where(CreditLedger.user_id == user.id)
   320	        .order_by(CreditLedger.created_at.desc())
   321	        .limit(1000)
   322	    )
   323	    credit_history = [
   324	        {
   325	            "delta": entry.delta,
   326	            "balance_after": entry.balance_after,
   327	            "reason": entry.reason,
   328	            "created_at": entry.created_at.isoformat() if entry.created_at else None,
   329	        }
   330	        for entry in ledger_rows.scalars()
   331	    ]
   332	
   333	    export_data = {
   334	        "exported_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
   335	        "user": profile,
   336	        "documents": documents,
   337	        "conversations": conversations,
   338	        "credit_history": credit_history,
   339	    }
   340	
   341	    _export_rate_limit[uid] = now
   342	    log_security_event("data_export", user_id=user.id)
   343	
   344	    content = json.dumps(export_data, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")
   345	    return Response(
   346	        content=content,

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Offset-preserving text normalization for the Quote Finder verifier (D2).
     2	
     3	The verifier matches an LLM-emitted quote against stored chunk text. Both
     4	sides are normalized with the IDENTICAL pipeline so quote/dash/ligature/width/
     5	whitespace variants align; a parallel index map then projects a match in
     6	normalized space back to RAW source offsets. The displayed quote is always the
     7	raw slice — never the normalized text and never the LLM emission — so the map
     8	must be exact.
     9	
    10	Two levels, selected by ``fuzzy``:
    11	
    12	* base (``fuzzy=False``)  — used by the exact-normalized tier. Folds only what
    13	  copy-paste / PDF extraction routinely varies (NFKC per code point, invisible
    14	  formatting chars, quote/dash/ellipsis, CJK width + corner quotes, whitespace
    15	  runs). Case and diacritics are PRESERVED.
    16	* fuzzy (``fuzzy=True``) — base plus ``casefold`` and combining-mark (Mn)
    17	  stripping (Arabic tashkeel etc.). Used only by the rapidfuzz alignment tier.
    18	
    19	All transforms are per-code-point so ``norm_to_raw[i]`` is the raw index that
    20	produced ``norm[i]``. A single raw code point may expand to several normalized
    21	chars (e.g. the ﬁ ligature → ``fi``, the … ellipsis → ``...``); every emitted
    22	char maps back to that one raw index.
    23	"""
    24	from __future__ import annotations
    25	
    26	import unicodedata
    27	from typing import List, Tuple
    28	
    29	# Punctuation folds applied per code point (after NFKC). NFKC already handles
    30	# full-width forms and NBSP, so this table only covers what NFKC leaves alone:
    31	# curly quotes, dash variants, the ellipsis char, and CJK corner quotes.
    32	_FOLD_MAP = {
    33	    "“": '"',  # “ left double quote
    34	    "”": '"',  # ” right double quote
    35	    "„": '"',  # „ low double quote
    36	    "«": '"',  # « guillemet
    37	    "»": '"',  # »
    38	    "‘": "'",  # ‘ left single quote
    39	    "’": "'",  # ’ right single quote / apostrophe
    40	    "‚": "'",  # ‚
    41	    "′": "'",  # ′ prime
    42	    "–": "-",  # – en dash
    43	    "—": "-",  # — em dash
    44	    "‐": "-",  # ‐ hyphen
    45	    "‑": "-",  # ‑ non-breaking hyphen
    46	    "‒": "-",  # ‒ figure dash
    47	    "―": "-",  # ― horizontal bar
    48	    "…": "...",  # … ellipsis
    49	    "「": '"',  # 「 corner bracket
    50	    "」": '"',  # 」
    51	    "『": '"',  # 『 white corner bracket
    52	    "』": '"',  # 』
    53	}
    54	
    55	# Invisible / decorative code points dropped in every mode.
    56	_DROP = {
    57	    "­",  # soft hyphen
    58	    "ـ",  # Arabic tatweel / kashida
    59	    "﻿",  # zero-width no-break space / BOM
    60	    "​",  # zero-width space
    61	}
    62	
    63	
    64	def _fold_codepoint(ch: str, *, fuzzy: bool) -> str | None:
    65	    """Return the normalized form of a single raw code point.
    66	
    67	    ``None`` signals "this is whitespace" (collapsed by the caller). ``""``
    68	    signals "drop entirely". Otherwise a 1+ char string is returned.
    69	    """
    70	    if ch in _DROP:
    71	        return ""
    72	    if ch.isspace():
    73	        return None
    74	
    75	    # NFKC per code point: folds ligatures (ﬁ→fi), full-width (３→3), NBSP, etc.
    76	    folded = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", ch)
    77	
    78	    out: List[str] = []
    79	    for c in folded:
    80	        if c in _DROP:
    81	            continue
    82	        if c in _FOLD_MAP:
    83	            out.append(_FOLD_MAP[c])
    84	            continue
    85	        if fuzzy and unicodedata.category(c) == "Mn":
    86	            # Combining mark (Arabic tashkeel, residual European accents):
    87	            # stripped only in the fuzzy tier.
    88	            continue
    89	        out.append(c.casefold() if fuzzy else c)
    90	    return "".join(out)
    91	
    92	
    93	def normalize(text: str, *, fuzzy: bool = False) -> Tuple[str, List[int]]:
    94	    """Normalize ``text`` and return ``(norm_text, norm_to_raw)``.
    95	
    96	    ``norm_to_raw[i]`` is the index in ``text`` of the raw code point that
    97	    produced ``norm_text[i]``. Whitespace runs collapse to a single space that
    98	    maps to the first whitespace code point of the run.
    99	    """
   100	    out_chars: List[str] = []
   101	    out_map: List[int] = []
   102	    prev_space = False
   103	
   104	    for raw_i, ch in enumerate(text):
   105	        folded = _fold_codepoint(ch, fuzzy=fuzzy)
   106	        if folded is None:  # whitespace
   107	            if prev_space:
   108	                continue
   109	            out_chars.append(" ")
   110	            out_map.append(raw_i)
   111	            prev_space = True
   112	            continue
   113	        if folded == "":  # dropped
   114	            continue
   115	        prev_space = False
   116	        for c in folded:
   117	            out_chars.append(c)
   118	            out_map.append(raw_i)
   119	
   120	    return "".join(out_chars), out_map
   121	
   122	
   123	def span_on_raw_boundaries(
   124	    norm_to_raw: List[int], norm_start: int, norm_end: int
   125	) -> bool:
   126	    """True iff ``[norm_start, norm_end)`` begins and ends at raw code-point
   127	    boundaries — i.e. the match spans WHOLE raw code points, not part of one
   128	    code point's multi-char expansion (… → ``...``, ﬁ → ``fi``, ㍿ → 株式会社).
   129	
   130	    A normalized match that ends inside an expansion does not correspond to a
   131	    real source substring, so the exact-normalized tier must reject it.
   132	    """
   133	    n = len(norm_to_raw)
   134	    if norm_start >= norm_end or norm_start < 0 or norm_end > n:
   135	        return False
   136	    start_ok = norm_start == 0 or norm_to_raw[norm_start] != norm_to_raw[norm_start - 1]
   137	    end_ok = norm_end == n or norm_to_raw[norm_end] != norm_to_raw[norm_end - 1]
   138	    return start_ok and end_ok
   139	
   140	
   141	def raw_span(
   142	    norm_to_raw: List[int], raw_len: int, norm_start: int, norm_end: int
   143	) -> Tuple[int, int]:
   144	    """Project a half-open normalized span ``[norm_start, norm_end)`` back to a
   145	    half-open RAW span. ``raw_len`` is ``len(original_text)``."""
   146	    if norm_start >= norm_end or not norm_to_raw:
   147	        return (0, 0)
   148	    raw_start = norm_to_raw[norm_start]
   149	    raw_end = norm_to_raw[norm_end - 1] + 1
   150	    return (raw_start, min(raw_end, raw_len))

   393	                    "error": "STRIPE_UNAVAILABLE",
   394	                    "message": "Failed to inspect active subscription",
   395	                },
   396	            )
   397	
   398	    if active_subscription_id:
   399	        try:
   400	            await asyncio.to_thread(
   401	                stripe.Subscription.cancel, active_subscription_id
   402	            )
   403	        except Exception as e:
   404	            log_security_event(
   405	                "stripe_cancel_failed",
   406	                user_id=user.id,
   407	                subscription_id=active_subscription_id,
   408	                error=str(e),
   409	            )
   410	            raise HTTPException(
   411	                status_code=502,
   412	                detail={
   413	                    "error": "STRIPE_UNAVAILABLE",
   414	                    "message": "Failed to cancel active subscription",
   415	                },
   416	            )
   417	
   418	    # 4) Delete user row (cascade handles accounts, credit_ledger, usage_records)
   419	    try:
   420	        await db.delete(user)
   421	        await db.commit()
   422	    except Exception:
   423	        # If deletion fails, return error
   424	        raise HTTPException(
   425	            status_code=500,
   426	            detail={"error": "SERVER_ERROR", "message": "Failed to delete user"},
   427	        )
   428	
   429	    # 5) Return confirmation
   430	    log_security_event("account_deleted", user_id=user.id, email=user.email)
   431	    return {"deleted": True}

    30	) -> User:
    31	    """Create a new user with signup bonus.
    32

    35	    """
    36	    # Check if user already exists
    37	    existing = await get_user_by_email(db, email)
    38	    if existing:
    39	        logger.warning("Attempted to create duplicate user: %s", email)
    40	        raise IntegrityError(
    41	            statement=None,
    42	            params=None,
    43	            orig=Exception(f"User with email {email} already exists"),
    44	        )
    45	
    46	    user = User(

    54	    )
    55	    db.add(user)

    57	    try:
    58	        await db.flush()

    61	        logger.warning("IntegrityError creating user: %s", email)

    68	        balance_after=settings.SIGNUP_BONUS_CREDITS,
    69	        reason="signup_bonus",
    70	    )
    71	    db.add(ledger)

+"""add saved_quotes (M3-B1, plan D8 as amended by §8.5 M3 / §8.1)
+
+User-saved verified quote cards. Rows snapshot a card's full trust state AT
+SAVE TIME (verification_tier/verification_score/verifier_version/
+source_kind, plus page/page_end/bboxes) — saved quotes must survive
+reparses (§8.1), so the display path reads these stored columns and never
+re-runs verify_quote. `source_chunk_id` is ON DELETE SET NULL, not CASCADE:
+the parse worker hard-deletes and recreates a document's chunks on every
+reparse, and a saved quote must outlive that.
+
+`quote_hash` (server-derived from normalized quote_text + verified page
+range — never client-supplied) backs the UNIQUE (user_id, document_id,
+quote_hash) constraint that makes POST .../quotes idempotent.
+

+class SavedQuote(Base):
+    """User-saved verified quote cards (M3-B1, plan D8 as amended by §8.5 M3
+    + §8.1's verification-persistence requirement).
+
+    Rows snapshot a card's full trust state AT SAVE TIME
+    (tier/score/verifier_version/source_kind, plus bboxes/page/page_end) —
+    saved quotes must survive/revalidate after reparses (§8.1), so the
+    display path reads these stored columns and NEVER re-runs verify_quote.
+    `source_chunk_id` is therefore ON DELETE SET NULL, not CASCADE: the
+    parse worker hard-deletes and recreates a document's chunks on every
+    reparse (parse_worker.py's `sa_delete(Chunk).where(...)`), and a saved
+    quote must outlive that — losing only the (informational) live chunk
+    link, never the row itself or its snapshotted display fields.
+
+    Cap enforcement (§8.4 point 2) counts ACTIVE rows per user ACROSS
+    documents — there is no soft-delete flag; DELETE is a real row removal,
+    which is what "delete frees a slot" means in the plan.
+
+    `quote_hash` is SERVER-derived (normalized quote_text + verified page
+    range, see saved_quotes_service.compute_quote_hash) — never trust a
+    client-supplied hash, or dedup/idempotency becomes forgeable.
+    """
+    __tablename__ = "saved_quotes"
+
+    id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
+        UUID(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, server_default=sa.text("gen_random_uuid()")
+    )
+    user_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
+        UUID(as_uuid=True), sa.ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False
+    )
+    document_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
+        UUID(as_uuid=True), sa.ForeignKey("documents.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False
+    )
+    page: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(sa.Integer, nullable=False)
+    page_end: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(sa.Integer, nullable=False)
+    quote_text: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(sa.Text, nullable=False)
+    bboxes: Mapped[Optional[list]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=True)
+    verification_tier: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(sa.String(16), nullable=False)
+    verification_score: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(sa.Float, nullable=False)
+    verifier_version: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(sa.String(16), nullable=False)
+    source_chunk_id: Mapped[Optional[uuid.UUID]] = mapped_column(
+        UUID(as_uuid=True), sa.ForeignKey("chunks.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
+    )
+    source_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(sa.String(16), nullable=False)
+    quote_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(sa.String(64), nullable=False)
+    note: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(sa.Text, nullable=True)
+    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
+        sa.DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False, server_default=sa.text("now()")
+    )
+    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
+        sa.DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False, server_default=sa.text("now()"), onupdate=sa.func.now()
+    )
+
+    __table_args__ = (
+        sa.Index("idx_saved_quotes_user_created", "user_id", "created_at"),
+        sa.Index("idx_saved_quotes_user_document", "user_id", "document_id"),
+        sa.UniqueConstraint(
+            "user_id", "document_id", "quote_hash", name="uq_saved_quotes_user_document_hash"
+        ),
+    )
+
+    document: Mapped["Document"] = relationship("Document")

    25	  if (reason === 'PRO_MODE_LIMIT_REACHED' || reason === 'BALANCED_MODE_LIMIT_REACHED' || reason === 'MODE_NOT_ALLOWED') {
    26	    return {
    27	      title: tOr('paywall.proMode.title', 'Keep using Pro analysis'),

    31	    };
    32	  }

    36	      title: tOr('paywall.layoutTranslation.title', 'Keep translating full PDFs'),
    37	      body: tOr('paywall.layoutTranslation.body', 'Free includes 2 layout-preserving PDF translations. Plus unlocks this workflow for active document work.'),
    38	      primaryLabel: tOr('paywall.layoutTranslation.cta', 'Upgrade for PDF translation'),
    39	      reason: 'layout_translation_limit',
    40	    };

    59	
    60	export function PaywallModal({ isOpen, onClose, reason, currentPlan }: PaywallModalProps) {
    61	  const { t, tOr } = useLocale();
    62	  const modalRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    63	  const copy = paywallCopy(reason, t, tOr);
    64	  const targetPlan = deriveUpgradePlan(currentPlan, reason ?? null);
    65	
    66	  useEffect(() => {
    67	    if (!isOpen) return;
    68	    const previouslyFocused = document.activeElement as HTMLElement;
    69	    const modal = modalRef.current;
    70	    if (!modal) return;
    71	
    72	    const focusableSelector = 'button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])';
    73	    const focusables = modal.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(focusableSelector);
    74	    focusables[0]?.focus();
    75	
    76	    function handleKeyDown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
    77	      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
    78	        onClose();
    79	        return;
    80	      }
    81	      if (e.key !== 'Tab') return;
    82	      const currentFocusables = modal!.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(focusableSelector);
    83	      const first = currentFocusables[0];
    84	      const last = currentFocusables[currentFocusables.length - 1];
    85	      if (e.shiftKey) {
    86	        if (document.activeElement === first) {
    87	          e.preventDefault();
    88	          last?.focus();
    89	        }
    90	      } else {
    91	        if (document.activeElement === last) {
    92	          e.preventDefault();
    93	          first?.focus();
    94	        }
    95	      }
    96	    }
    97	
    98	    modal.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    99	    return () => {
   100	      modal.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
   101	      previouslyFocused?.focus();
   102	    };
   103	  }, [isOpen, onClose]);
   104	
   105	  if (!isOpen) return null;
   106	
   107	  return (
   108	    <div
   109	      ref={modalRef}
   110	      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 animate-fade-in motion-reduce:animate-none overscroll-contain"
   111	      onClick={onClose}
   112	      tabIndex={-1}
   113	    >
   114	      <div
   115	        className="bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-xl p-6 max-w-md w-full mx-4 shadow-xl animate-slide-up motion-reduce:animate-none"
   116	        role="dialog"
   117	        aria-modal="true"
   118	        aria-labelledby="paywall-title"
   119	        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
   120	      >
   121	        <h2 id="paywall-title" className="text-xl font-semibold mb-4 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
   122	          {copy.title}

   109	@documents_router.get("", response_model=list[DocumentBrief])
   110	async def list_documents(
   111	    limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=100),
   112	    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
   113	    user: Optional[User] = Depends(get_current_user_optional),
   114	    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db_session),
   115	):
   116	    """List current user's documents. Returns empty list for anonymous users."""
   117	    if not user:
   118	        return []
   119	
   120	    from sqlalchemy import select
   121	
   122	    from app.models.tables import Document
   123	
   124	    result = await db.execute(
   125	        select(Document)
   126	        .where(Document.user_id == user.id)
   127	        .where(Document.status != "deleting")
   128	        .order_by(Document.created_at.desc())
   129	        .limit(limit)
   130	        .offset(offset)
   131	    )
   132	    docs = result.scalars().all()
   133	    return [
   134	        DocumentBrief(
   135	            id=str(d.id),
   136	            filename=d.filename,
   137	            status=d.status,
   138	            created_at=d.created_at.isoformat() if d.created_at else None,
   139	        )
   140	        for d in docs
   141	    ]

    27	export default function SavedQuoteCard({ quote, index, biblio, onJump, onDeleted }: SavedQuoteCardProps) {
    28	  const { tOr } = useLocale();
    29	  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
    30	  const [note, setNote] = useState(quote.note || '');
    31	  const [savingNote, setSavingNote] = useState(false);
    32	  const [noteError, setNoteError] = useState(false);

    35	  // The quote object is the parent's source of truth; re-sync the local

    65	      setNoteError(true);
    66	    } finally {

    23	export default function QuoteResultCard({ card, index, documentId, biblio, onJump, onSaveLimitReached }: QuoteResultCardProps) {
    24	  const { tOr } = useLocale();
    25	  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
    26	  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
    27	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
    28	
    29	  const handleCopy = async () => {
    30	    const apaInText = formatApaInText(biblio, card.page);
    31	    const text = `"${card.displayText}" ${apaInText}`;
    32	    try {
    33	      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    34	      setCopied(true);
    35	      window.setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    36	    } catch {
    37	      // Clipboard can be blocked in non-secure contexts / permission
    38	      // denial — best-effort, no toast (the missing "Copied" state is
    39	      // itself the "didn't work" cue, matching MessageBubble's copy button).
    40	    }
    41	  };
    42	
    43	  const handleSave = async () => {
    44	    // Idempotent at the UI level too: once this card instance is marked
    45	    // saved locally, further clicks no-op without a network round trip
    46	    // (the backend is itself idempotent — re-POSTing returns the existing
    47	    // row — this just avoids the redundant call).
    48	    if (saved || saving) return;
    49	    setSaving(true);
    50	    try {
    51	      await saveQuote(documentId, { chunkId: card.chunkId, quoteText: card.displayText, pageHint: card.page });
    52	      setSaved(true);
    53	    } catch (err) {
    54	      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 403 && err.code === 'SAVED_QUOTES_LIMIT_REACHED') {
    55	        const limit = typeof err.detail.limit === 'number' ? err.detail.limit : 30;
    56	        const plan = typeof err.detail.plan === 'string' ? err.detail.plan : 'free';

## Overall verdict: REVISE

### High

1. **The 30-quote cap is bypassable with concurrent distinct saves.**  
   [quotes.py:531](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/api/quotes.py:531) checks the count before [save_quote commits the insert](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/services/saved_quotes_service.py:121), without a per-user lock or serializable transaction. Starting at 29 rows, multiple requests with different verified quotes can all observe 29 and commit, exceeding 30. The unique constraint protects only identical hashes. The real-Postgres concurrency test covers identical saves, not distinct saves at the cap.

   Serialize new saves per user, then re-check idempotency and count before inserting in the same transaction. Add a real-Postgres test with concurrent distinct quotes at 29 rows.

### Medium

2. **The GDPR/data-portability export omits saved quotes and user-authored notes.**  
   [users.py:245](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/api/users.py:245) claims to export all user data, but its payload at [users.py:333](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/api/users.py:333) contains no `SavedQuote` rows. Notes can contain personal or sensitive information. Account deletion is covered by `user_id ON DELETE CASCADE`, but portability is incomplete.

3. **Successful note saves never update the client’s confirmed value.**  
   [SavedQuoteCard.tsx:53](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/SavedQuoteCard.tsx:53) always compares and reverts against `quote.note`, while the successful PATCH response at line 60 is discarded and the parent row is not updated. Consequently:

   - Every later blur can issue another PATCH for an unchanged note.
   - A later network failure reverts to the pre-first-save value, not the last confirmed value.
   - Overlapping blur requests can commit out of order and overwrite newer text.

   Track a confirmed note from the PATCH response and serialize or generation-guard overlapping saves.

4. **“Saved” state can transfer to a different quote after another search.**  
   Cards are keyed only by `chunkId-index` at [QuoteCardList.tsx:108](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/QuoteCardList.tsx:108). If a later search returns different text from the same chunk at the same position, React reuses the component and preserves `saved=true`. The guard at [QuoteResultCard.tsx:43](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/QuoteResultCard.tsx:43) then prevents saving the new quote. Include page/text identity in the key or reset local state when card identity changes.

5. **`source_chunk_id` lacks an index for the reparse FK action.**  
   Migration 0036 defines `ON DELETE SET NULL` at [0036:45](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/alembic/versions/20260802_0036_add_saved_quotes.py:45), but its indexes at lines 52–56 omit `source_chunk_id`. PostgreSQL does not automatically index referencing FK columns. The parse worker bulk-deletes chunks at [parse_worker.py:184](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/workers/parse_worker.py:184); once `saved_quotes` grows, referential-action lookups can repeatedly scan the table. Add an index, preferably partial on non-null `source_chunk_id`.

### Low

6. **A failed Saved-tab refresh renders stale cached rows.**  
   [QuoteFinderPanel.tsx:126](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/QuoteFinderPanel.tsx:126) starts a new fetch without clearing or keying `savedQuotes`/`savedGlobalCount`; the error path leaves both untouched. Once loading ends, [the renderer](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Quotes/QuoteFinderPanel.tsx:345) displays the error and the prior rows. After document or plan retargeting, those can belong to the previous context.

7. **Profile document-name resolution is incomplete.**  
   [SavedQuotesSection.tsx:145](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/frontend/src/components/Profile/SavedQuotesSection.tsx:145) joins quotes against one default `getMyDocuments()` request. That endpoint returns only 50 owned documents ([documents.py:109](/Users/mayijie/Projects/Code/010_DocTalk/backend/app/api/documents.py:109)) and excludes demo documents. Saved demo quotes and Pro quotes from older documents therefore appear as “Untitled document.” Return the filename with the saved-quote feed or perform a complete paginated join.

No external trust forgery or ownership bypass was found: document access, chunk/document binding, server-derived trust fields, `page_hint`, and owner-only PATCH/DELETE are correctly enforced. React rendering escapes quote/note content, the 2,000-character note cap is enforced, all 20 new keys exist across 11 locales, and migrations 0036/0037 are otherwise add-only and metadata-safe.
